# Bloom language nodes

Every node kind the language has (432), in the spec's order: the constructors that make it, the token sets its `.size()`, `.tint()`, `.style()`, `.shape()` and `.density()` read, and the modifiers it takes beyond the universal ones. Unless a node says otherwise it also takes every universal modifier ([`.padding()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#padding), [`.background()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#background), [`.onPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onpress)…; the list is in [the modifiers reference](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#universal-modifiers)).

This page is generated from `packages/bloom-language/spec/language.json` and the package's types, the same spec the compiler checks a chain against, so what it lists is exactly what builds. For how to use them, read [the tour](bloom-language-tour.mdx).

| [rowActionButton](#rowactionbutton) | `RowActionButton` |
| [swipeRow](#swiperow) | `SwipeRow` |
| [themeToggle](#themetoggle) | `ThemeToggle` |

## Nodes

### `stack`

- `HStack(...children: Child[])`: Children side by side, in reading order.
- `VStack(...children: Child[])`: Children top to bottom.

Modifiers:

- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between children.
- [`.horizontalSpacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#horizontalspacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between children side by side (`columnGap`, Tailwind's `gap-x-*`), over `spacing`'s.
- [`.verticalSpacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#verticalspacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between children one above another, and between wrapped lines (`rowGap`, Tailwind's `gap-y-*`), over `spacing`'s.
- [`.childSpacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#childspacing) `(axis: Axis, space: SpaceToken)`: Tailwind's `space-x-*`/`space-y-*` exactly as NativeWind draws them: on the web a margin after every child but the last, along `axis` (the inline end, or the block end); on Android and iOS nothing, as NativeWind writes no rule for a child selector. Not a gap: new screens use `.spacing()`.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: Alignment)`: The children's place across the axis.
- [`.justify()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#justify) `(value: Justify)`: How the children spread along the axis: packed at `start`, `center` or `end`, or the free space `between`, `around` or `evenly` around them.
- [`.wrap()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#wrap) `(value?: boolean)`: Children that do not fit flow onto further lines.
- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: Lays the children out along `horizontal` or `vertical`, over the constructor's own (a breakpoint variant's row).
- [`.hoverHighlight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hoverhighlight) `(active?: boolean)`: Bloom's hover wash behind the stack (`SubtleHover`): under the pointer, on the web; or while `active` (a coordinated highlight), on every platform.
- [`.listMarker()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#listmarker) `(value: ListMarker)`: Web: a list, each child a row behind the marker (an HTML list and its items, `list-style-type`); give it the room for the markers with a leading padding. Native draws no markers, as React Native has none.

And every universal modifier.

### `zstack`

- `ZStack(...children: Child[])`: Children over one another, later ones on top: the stack is as big as its largest child, and each child sits at its `.alignment()` (`center`).

Modifiers:

- [`.alignment()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#alignment) `(value: OverlayAlignment)`: Where each child sits in the stack (`ZStack(alignment:)`): `center` by default.
- [`.hoverHighlight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hoverhighlight) `(active?: boolean)`: Bloom's hover wash behind the stack (`SubtleHover`): under the pointer, on the web; or while `active` (a coordinated highlight), on every platform.

And every universal modifier.

### `spacer`

- `Spacer()`: Takes the free space along its stack's axis.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `text`

- `Text(...runs: TextRun[])`: Text. Runs are joined in order; a nested `Text` is an inline span with its own modifiers.
- `RawText(...runs: TextRun[])`: React Native's own `Text`: what its chain names and nothing else, no Bloom family, size or ink, as a span is. It is what a screen moved from React Native's `Text` draws, so the move changes no pixel; new screens use `Text`.

Modifiers:

- [`.lines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#lines) `(value: number)`: At most `n` lines; what does not fit is cut where `truncation` says (the tail by default).
- [`.noWrap()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#nowrap) `()`: Web: never wraps (`white-space: nowrap`); native text in a row already keeps its width.
- [`.truncation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#truncation) `(value: Truncation)`: Where a text cut by `lines` loses its characters: `head`, `middle`, `tail` or `clip`.
- [`.selectable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selectable) `(value?: boolean)`: The reader can select and copy the text.
- [`.textStyle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#textstyle) `(value: TextStyleName)`: One of Bloom's typography components: `title`, `subtitle`, `h1`…`h6`, `paragraph`, `lead`, `large`, `small`, `muted`, `blockquote`. Its role, rule and spacing come with it, as the component draws them.
- [`.link()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#link) `(href: string)`: A link to `href`, drawn as Bloom's inline link (the primary ink, underlined, announced as a link): an anchor on the web, opened outside the app on native. Its own `.foreground()` replaces the ink; `.onPress()` replaces the opening.
- [`.textAlign()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#textalign) `(value: TextAlignment)`: How its lines align: `start` and `end` follow the reading direction; `left` and `right` are Tailwind's `text-left` and `text-right` as NativeWind writes them (React Native's own `left` and `right`, the web's CSS ones).
- [`.italic()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#italic) `()`
- [`.underline()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#underline) `()`
- [`.strikethrough()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#strikethrough) `()`
- [`.textCase()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#textcase) `(value: TextCase)`: Draws the text in upper case, lower case, or with each word capitalised.
- [`.monospacedDigit()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#monospaceddigit) `()`: Digits of one width, so numbers in a column or a counter line up (`font-variant: tabular-nums`).
- [`.wordBreak()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#wordbreak) `(value: WordBreak)`: Web: where a line may break inside a word (`all`, `words`); native text keeps its platform's breaking.
- [`.textShadow()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#textshadow) `(value: TextShadowName)`: A shadow under the glyphs, each named by its exact CSS `text-shadow` (`'0 1px 3px rgb(0 0 0 / 0.8)'`): React Native's `textShadowColor`, `textShadowOffset` and `textShadowRadius`, the web's `text-shadow`. For text over a photo or a video.
- [`.contentTransition()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contenttransition) `(value: ContentTransition, direction?: number)`: How a change of the text inside an animation plays, up when `direction` rose and down when it fell: `roll` rolls the old text out and the new one in; `numeric` (SwiftUI's `.contentTransition(.numericText(value:))`) rolls each character that changed, the rest staying put.

And every universal modifier.

### `icon`

- `Icon(icon: IconComponent)`: A Bloom icon component (`RiHeartLine`, `ReplyIcon`…), drawn at a size token in the current foreground.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [iconSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#iconsize), `'2xs'`, `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`, `'xl'`, `'2xl'`, `'3xl'`, `'10'`, `'11'`, `'13'`, `'14'`, `'15'`, `'17'`, `'18'`, `'21'`, `'22'`, `'40'`, `'44'`, `'64'`, `'96'`

Modifiers:

- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: IconSizeToken)`

And every universal modifier.

### `avatar`

- `Avatar(source: string | null | undefined, options: AvatarOptions = {})`: Bloom `Avatar`. `source` is a URL or a file id the app's `ImageResolver` resolves.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [avatarSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#avatarsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`, `'xl'`
- `.tint()`: [avatarColor](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#avatarcolor), `'neutral'`, `'blue'`, `'lime'`, `'pink'`
- `.shape()`: [shapeName](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#shapename), `'circle'`, `'squircle'`, `'square'`, `'slanted'`, `'arch'`, `'semicircle'`, `'oval'`, `'pill'`, `'triangle'`, `'arrow'`, `'fan'`, `'diamond'`, `'clamshell'`, `'pentagon'`, `'gem'`, `'very-sunny'`, `'sunny'`, `'4-sided-cookie'`, `'6-sided-cookie'`, `'7-sided-cookie'`, `'9-sided-cookie'`, `'12-sided-cookie'`, `'4-leaf-clover'`, `'8-leaf-clover'`, `'burst'`, `'soft-burst'`, `'boom'`, `'soft-boom'`, `'flower'`, `'puffy'`, `'puffy-diamond'`, `'ghost-ish'`, `'pixel-circle'`, `'pixel-triangle'`, `'bun'`, `'heart'`

Modifiers:

- [`.motion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#motion) `(value: AvatarMotion)`: Whether its character moves (Bloom's `motion`): `auto` (the default), `always`, `never` (a still portrait); reduced motion always wins.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: AvatarSizeToken)`: A rung (`xs` 20, `sm` 24, `md` 32, `lg` 36) or an app's own diameter; 40 with none.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: AvatarColor)`: The initials disc's tint; derived from the name when not set.
- [`.shape()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shape) `(value: ShapeName)`: One of Bloom's silhouettes; the image and the fallback keep it.
- [`.verified()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#verified) `(value?: boolean)`: Bloom's verified mark in the corner.
- [`.live()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#live) `(value?: boolean)`: Live-streaming: the live ring, and the LIVE pill unless `liveBadge(false)`.
- [`.liveBadge()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#livebadge) `(value: boolean)`
- [`.presence()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presence) `(value: Presence)`: A presence dot on its edge (Bloom `AvatarPresence`), sized from the avatar.
- [`.cornerRadius()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#cornerradius) `(value: Exclude<CornerName, 'concentric'>)`: The radius of its corners (Bloom's Avatar `style.borderRadius`): a rounded square of an avatar a shape name does not draw (a list's 12pt corners on a 58pt cover). `.corner()` is a box around it instead.

And every universal modifier.

### `avatarGroup`

- `AvatarGroup(items: readonly AvatarGroupItem[])`: Bloom `AvatarGroup`: its members (Bloom's `AvatarGroupItem`: `uri`, `name`, `initials`…), first on top.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [avatarSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#avatarsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`, `'xl'`
- `.shape()`: [shapeName](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#shapename), `'circle'`, `'squircle'`, `'square'`, `'slanted'`, `'arch'`, `'semicircle'`, `'oval'`, `'pill'`, `'triangle'`, `'arrow'`, `'fan'`, `'diamond'`, `'clamshell'`, `'pentagon'`, `'gem'`, `'very-sunny'`, `'sunny'`, `'4-sided-cookie'`, `'6-sided-cookie'`, `'7-sided-cookie'`, `'9-sided-cookie'`, `'12-sided-cookie'`, `'4-leaf-clover'`, `'8-leaf-clover'`, `'burst'`, `'soft-burst'`, `'boom'`, `'soft-boom'`, `'flower'`, `'puffy'`, `'puffy-diamond'`, `'ghost-ish'`, `'pixel-circle'`, `'pixel-triangle'`, `'bun'`, `'heart'`

Modifiers:

- [`.layout()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#layout) `(value: AvatarLayout)`
- [`.motion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#motion) `(value: AvatarMotion)`: Each member's character motion (Bloom's `motion`).
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: AvatarSizeToken)`: Each avatar's diameter (a cluster's whole box); Bloom's default with none.
- [`.shape()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shape) `(value: ShapeName)`
- [`.max()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#max) `(value: number)`: The most avatars before the `+N` chip.
- [`.total()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#total) `(value: number | undefined)`: The real number of members, when more exist than the items given.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between avatars of a `row`.
- [`.overflow()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#overflow) `(value: AvatarOverflow)`
- [`.initials()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#initials) `()`: A member without an image shows its tinted initial, not the neutral placeholder.
- [`.onPressItem()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onpressitem) `(value: ((item: AvatarGroupItem, index: number) => void) | undefined)`
- [`.hoverCard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hovercard) `(value?: boolean)`: Web: hovering an avatar shows the member's card.
- [`.ringColor()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ringcolor) `(value: InkToken)`: The ring each face is cut out of its neighbours by (Bloom's `ringColor`): the colour behind the group, a role or an app colour (`'background'`).
- [`.itemAction()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#itemaction) `(value: (item: AvatarGroupItem, index: number) => Child)`: Web: a view under the member's name in its hover card (Bloom's `renderItemAction`: a follow button).
- [`.variant()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#variant) `(value: string)` (here: a value): The rendition the members' images load (Bloom's `variant`: `'thumb'` with none, `'small'`, `'medium'`).

And every universal modifier.

### `badge`

- `Badge(content?: string | number)`: Bloom `Badge`: a count or a label (a dot with none, `.dot()`). Attach it with `.badge(Badge(…))`.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [badgeSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#badgesize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`, `'label-small'`, `'label-medium'`
- `.tint()`: [tone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tone), `'neutral'`, `'accent'`, `'vivid'`, `'support'`, `'action'`, `'success'`, `'warning'`, `'danger'`, `'info'`
- `.style()`: [badgeStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#badgestyle), `'solid'`, `'subtle'`, `'outlined'`, `'onMedia'`

Modifiers:

- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: BadgeStyle)`
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: BloomTone)`
- [`.color()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#color) `(value: AccentTone)`: Bloom's semantic colour (`color`): one of its accent tones, the tone when no `tint()` is set.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BadgeSize)`
- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: IconComponent)`: A glyph before the label, at the rung's size, in the label's ink.
- [`.dot()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dot) `()`: A dot without content: a status dot alone, a marker when attached.
- [`.max()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#max) `(value: number)`: Counts above it read `{max}+`.
- [`.placement()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#placement) `(value: BadgePlacement)`: Where an attached badge sits on its view.

And every universal modifier.

### `iconCircle`

- `IconCircle(icon: IconComponent)`: Bloom `IconCircle`: an icon on a tinted disc.

Token sets:

And every universal modifier.

### `skeleton`

- `Skeleton(shape: SkeletonShape)`: A Bloom skeleton shape, sized by its frame (a `text` line by its font).

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `skeletonGroup`

- `SkeletonGroup(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Skeleton.Region`: the shapes of what is loading, announced once.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `image`

- `Image(source: BloomImageSource | null | undefined, alt?: string)`: A raster image, drawn by Bloom's `BloomImage`. `source` is a URL, a bundled asset (`require()`) or a URI descriptor; `alt` names it for assistive technology.

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [colorRole](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#colorrole), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.contentFit()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentfit) `(value: ContentFit)`: How the image fills its box. Defaults to `cover`.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorName)`: Paints every opaque pixel one colour role (monochrome artwork).
- [`.crossFade()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#crossfade) `(value: DurationName)`: Cross-fades the image in when it arrives, over a Bloom duration. Defaults to none. (`.transition()` is how any view arrives and leaves.)
- [`.onLoad()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onload) `(value: ImageLoadHandler | undefined)`: The intrinsic pixel size, once loaded.
- [`.onError()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onerror) `(value: ImageErrorHandler | undefined)`

And every universal modifier.

### `fill`

- `Fill(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Fill`: its children cover the whole of the view it is in.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `ratio`

- `AspectRatio(ratio: number, ...children: Child[])`: Bloom `AspectRatio`: the width of its parent and the height `ratio` (width over height) gives it, so a media well keeps its size before it loads.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `grid`

- `Grid(...cells: Child[])`: Bloom `ResponsiveGrid`: its cells in as many equal columns as fit.

Modifiers:

- [`.columns()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#columns) `(value: number)`: The most columns (Bloom's default: 2).
- [`.minCellWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#mincellwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: A cell's narrowest width before the grid drops a column (Bloom's default: 320).
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The space between cells, both ways.
- [`.flow()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#flow) `(value: GridFlow)`: `rows` (default) aligns cells in rows; `columns` stacks each column.
- [`.stretchItems()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stretchitems) `(value?: boolean)`: Chart cards fill the tallest cell of each row of several columns (Bloom's `stretchItems`).

And every universal modifier.

### `button`

- `Button(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Button`. An `Icon` child is its glyph; `Text` children are its label.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [controlSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#controlsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`
- `.tint()`: [tone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tone), `'neutral'`, `'accent'`, `'vivid'`, `'support'`, `'action'`, `'success'`, `'warning'`, `'danger'`, `'info'`
- `.style()`: [buttonStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#buttonstyle), `'solid'`, `'subtle'`, `'outline'`, `'plain'`, `'glass'`, `'glassProminent'`
- `.shape()`: [buttonShape](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#buttonshape), `'circle'`, `'pill'`, `'fit'`
- `.density()`: `'default'`, `'compact'`

Modifiers:

- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: ButtonStyle)`: Bloom's appearance, or a glass style: `glass` (the button on glass, its ink the neutral tone's) and `glassProminent` (on glass tinted with its tone's fill, the accent by default: one per bar, for the key action).
- [`.shape()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shape) `(value: ButtonShape)`
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: BloomTone)`
- [`.loading()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#loading) `(value?: boolean)`: Busy: the label gives way to Bloom's spinner and presses are ignored.
- [`.pressed()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#pressed) `(value: boolean)`: A toggle, on or off: announced as pressed on every platform.
- [`.link()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#link) `(href: string)`: A link: a real anchor on the web, the address opened on native (unless `.onPress` is set).
- [`.lines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#lines) `(value: number)`: Truncates the label to `n` lines instead of wrapping it.
- [`.underline()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#underline) `()`: The label carries its underline at rest (a `plain` action inside running copy).
- [`.colors()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#colors) `(background: ColorToken, foreground: ColorToken)`: A paired fill and label from two colour roles (Bloom's `colors`), for a brand action; the geometry stays the button's.
- [`.linkTone()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#linktone) `(value: ButtonLinkTone)`: A `plain` link's ink: the accent (`primary`), the secondary text, or the reading colour (`text`).
- [`.inverted()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#inverted) `(value?: boolean)`: The theme's background and foreground swapped: a high-contrast action.
- [`.density()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#density) `(value: Density<'button'>)`: The control's geometry over the nearest Bloom scope's: `default` or `compact`.
- [`.iconSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#iconsize) `(value: IconSizeToken)`: Its glyph's size, an icon rung (`xl` is 28) or a numeric size (`'22'`), over its size's own (Bloom's `iconSize`).
- [`.stopPropagation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stoppropagation) `(value?: boolean)`: Its press goes no further on the web: a button inside a pressable row presses only itself (Bloom's `stopPropagation`). Native presses never bubble.
- [`.loadingIndicator()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#loadingindicator) `(value: ViewNode | null)`: Its spinner while loading: a view, or `null` for none, the label or glyph staying (Bloom's `loadingIndicator`).
- [`.hoverForeground()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hoverforeground) `(value: ColorToken | undefined)`: Its ink under the pointer, a colour role (Bloom's `hoverColor`); its ink at rest is `.foreground(role)` (Bloom's `color`); `undefined` is Bloom's own.

And every universal modifier.

Modifiers:

- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`: Every button's size.
- [`.dividers()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dividers) `(value?: boolean)`: The hairlines between controls (on by default).
- [`.separated()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#separated) `(value?: boolean)`: Flat segments with a gap between them, under the shared outer pill.

And every universal modifier.

### `fab`

- `FAB(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Fab`, on the platform's glass: the lens on Android and OxyOS, `glassEffect` on iOS 26 (blur before it), Bloom's frosted material on the web. An `Icon` child is its glyph; a `Text` child is its extended label. `.glass(...)` sets the material; without it, the FAB is tinted with its own solid fill.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [controlSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#controlsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`
- `.tint()`: [tone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tone), `'neutral'`, `'accent'`, `'vivid'`, `'support'`, `'action'`, `'success'`, `'warning'`, `'danger'`, `'info'`
- `.style()`: [appearance](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#appearance), `'solid'`, `'subtle'`, `'outline'`, `'plain'`
- `.shape()`: [fabShape](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#fabshape), `'circle'`, `'rounded'`, `'squircle'`

Modifiers:

- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: BloomAppearance)`
- [`.shape()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shape) `(value: FabShape)`: The silhouette: `squircle` (the default, a rounded square with Apple's continuous corner), `rounded` (a rounded square) or `circle`. Its glass, the press and a menu's morph take the same shape.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: BloomTone)`
- [`.collapsed()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#collapsed) `(value?: boolean)`: Folds the extended label away, keeping the action and its name.

And every universal modifier.

### `menu`

- `Menu(trigger: Child, ...rows: MenuChild[])`: A menu: `trigger` opens it, and `rows` are what it presents. Each platform presents it natively: the lens menu on Android and OxyOS, the system menu on iOS, Bloom's menu on the web.

Modifiers:

- [`.actions()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#actions) `(value: MenuActionsView)`: What its tagged rows do: an `actions()` view, whose body (it may call hooks) runs only when a row is chosen. The rows stay plain data, which every platform can present before the menu opens.
- [`.menuStyle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#menustyle) `(value: MenuStyle)`: `dropdown`: Bloom's own DropdownMenu on every platform (its panel and rows, as an app's `<DropdownMenu>` draws them), not the platform's menu (the lens menu on Android and OxyOS, SwiftUI's on iOS). `platform` is the default.
- [`.presented()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presented) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: The menu open through a binding, SwiftUI's `isPresented`: the app opens it (setting `true`) as its trigger's press does, and hears it close (`false`). The system menu on iOS opens only from its trigger, so a presented menu is Bloom's menu there.

And every universal modifier.

### `menuItem`

- `Item(title: string)`: A menu row: a title, and what choosing it does (`.onPress`).

Modifiers:

- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: IconComponent)`
- [`.iconSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#iconsize) `(value: IconSizeToken)`: The glyph's size: an icon rung or a numeric size (`'22'`), over the menu's 20pt. Bloom's menus and the lens menu draw it; the system menu on iOS draws its own.
- [`.iconForeground()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#iconforeground) `(value: InkToken)`: The glyph's ink, a colour role, over the menu's (its quiet ink, or the negative one of a destructive row). Bloom's menus and the lens menu draw it; the system menu on iOS draws its own.
- [`.subtitle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#subtitle) `(value: string | undefined)`: A second, quieter line under the title (drawn by the lens menu on Android and OxyOS).
- [`.destructive()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#destructive) `()`: Drawn in the theme's negative colour: an action that deletes or leaves.
- [`.disabled()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#disabled) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.checked()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#checked) `(value: boolean)`: A check mark: the row is a toggle that is on.
- [`.keepOpen()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keepopen) `()`: Choosing the row leaves the menu open.
- [`.onPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onpress) `(value: (() => void) | undefined)`
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.tag()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tag) `(value: string)`: The row's action by name: the menu's `actions()` resolve it when the row is chosen.
- [`.shortcut()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shortcut) `(value: string)`: The key chord that runs it from a menubar (`Mod+N`, `Mod+Shift+Z`), drawn beside its title there.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `menuSubmenu`

- `Submenu(title: string, ...rows: MenuChild[])`: A row that opens its own rows beside (or, on a phone, in place of) the menu. One level deep.

Modifiers:

- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: IconComponent)`
- [`.iconSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#iconsize) `(value: IconSizeToken)`: The glyph's size: an icon rung or a numeric size (`'22'`), over the menu's 20pt, as an `Item`'s. Bloom's menus draw it; the lens menu's submenus and the system menu on iOS draw their own.
- [`.iconForeground()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#iconforeground) `(value: InkToken)`: The glyph's ink, a colour role, over the menu's quiet ink, as an `Item`'s. Bloom's menus draw it; the lens menu's submenus and the system menu on iOS draw their own.
- [`.disabled()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#disabled) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `menuSection`

- `Section(...rows: MenuChild[])`: Rows that belong together, set apart from the rows around them.

Modifiers:

- [`.title()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#title) `(value: string)`: A quiet heading above the section's rows.
- [`.selection()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selection) `(value: readonly [value: T | undefined, set: (value: T) => void])`: One of the section's rows chosen, as SwiftUI's `Picker` in a `Menu` (a `UIMenu` of single selection): each tagged `Item` in it is a choice whose tag is its value, marked while the binding holds it; choosing one sets the binding. `undefined` is no row chosen. Bloom's menus draw a radio group (`DropdownMenuRadioGroup`), the lens menu a check on the chosen row, iOS the system's inline picker.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `divider`

- `Divider(label?: Child)`: Bloom `Divider`: a line across its stack (vertical in an `HStack`), with an optional label on it. In a menu, the line between rows.

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [colorRole](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#colorrole), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.lineStyle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#linestyle) `(value: DividerStyle)`: `single` (default) hairline, `double` framing hairlines, or a `fill` strip.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: 'start' | 'center' | 'end')` (here: [dividerAlign](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#divideralign)): Where the label sits on the line.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The space on each side of the line, along its stack.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorName)`: The line's colour role; the neutral separator by default.
- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: Which way the line runs, over its stack's (vertical in an `HStack`, horizontal elsewhere): `.axis('vertical')` is a vertical line outside a row (Bloom `Divider vertical`), as in a `ZStack` or an `Embed`'s view.

And every universal modifier.

### `alert`

- `Alert(title: string)`: An alert, presented with `.alert(isPresented, Alert(title))`: Bloom's `AlertDialog`, its confirm and cancel actions closing it.

Modifiers:

- [`.message()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#message) `(value: string)`: The text under the title.
- [`.confirmLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#confirmlabel) `(value: string)`: The confirm button's label (Bloom's localized "Confirm" without it).
- [`.cancelLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#cancellabel) `(value: string)`: The cancel button's label (Bloom's localized "Cancel" without it).
- [`.onConfirm()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onconfirm) `(value: (() => void) | undefined)`: Runs when the confirm button is pressed; the alert then closes.
- [`.onCancel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#oncancel) `(value: (() => void) | undefined)`: Runs when the alert is cancelled: the cancel button, Escape or the backdrop.
- [`.destructive()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#destructive) `()`: The confirm button in the destructive tone.
- [`.hideCancel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hidecancel) `()`: Only the confirm button: an acknowledgement.
- [`.defaultAction()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#defaultaction) `(value: AlertDefault)`: The action that takes the initial focus and Enter.
- [`.dismissible()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dismissible) `(value: boolean)`: `false`: Escape and the backdrop do not dismiss it.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: ViewNode)`: A view above the title (the application's icon).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `dialog`

- `Dialog(...content: Child[])`: A dialog of `content`, presented with `.dialog(isPresented, Dialog(...))`, or a view of its own with `.presented(isPresented)`: Bloom's `Dialog`, from the bottom and centred from `md` as it places itself, or where `.presentAt()` says.

Modifiers:

- [`.title()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#title) `(value: string)`
- [`.message()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#message) `(value: string)`: The text under the title.
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: The dialog's accessible name, when it has no title.
- [`.buttons()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#buttons) `(value: readonly DialogAction[])`: The action row: Bloom's `DialogAction`s, in order.
- [`.presentAt()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presentat) `(base: DialogPlacement, tiers?: DialogTiers)`: Where it presents: `base`, and from each breakpoint `tiers` names up (Bloom's responsive placement).
- [`.dismissOnBackdrop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dismissonbackdrop) `(value: boolean)`: `false`: a press on the backdrop does not dismiss it.
- [`.scrollable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollable) `(value?: boolean)`: Its content scrolls when it is taller than the dialog.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.header()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#header) `(value: DialogHeaderConfig)`: Bloom's dialog header: a collapsing title, its slots and its layout.
- [`.footer()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footer) `(content: Child)`: A view pinned under the content (Bloom's `footer`).
- [`.page()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#page) `(value: DialogScreenFrame)`: A page of a dialog that navigates (Bloom's `frame`: its key, header and travel).
- [`.frame()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#frame) `(value: DialogFrame)`: Its width and its ceiling.
- [`.padding()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#padding) `(value: SpaceToken)` (here: [space](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#space)): The padding around its content (Bloom's `contentPadding`).
- [`.sideGutter()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sidegutter) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The least room kept beside it on a narrow screen (Bloom's `minSideGutter`).
- [`.maxHeightRatio()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#maxheightratio) `(value: number)`: Its height ceiling, as a fraction of the window's (`0.9`).
- [`.onDismissed()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ondismissed) `(action: () => void)`: Once it has finished closing, its exit animation done (Bloom's `onDismissed`): after its binding turned false and it left the screen, the place to unmount what presented it.
- [`.presented()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presented) `(isPresented: Binding<boolean>)`: The dialog as a view of its own, shown while `isPresented` holds; dismissing it sets it false. It ends the chain: what a component that is a dialog returns, its binding handed in by the caller.
- [`.control()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#control) `(control: DialogControlProps)`: The dialog as a view of its own, opened and closed by Bloom's dialog control (`useDialogControl()`: `control.open()`, `control.close()`), for a dialog another view opens imperatively. It ends the chain, as `.presented()` does.
- [`.detached()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#detached) `(value?: boolean)`: Floats a bottom dialog above the window's safe area, with rounded corners and gutters beside it (Bloom's `detached`); other placements are as they were.
- [`.panelFrame()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#panelframe) `(value: FrameSpec)`: The side or bottom panel's own frame (Bloom's `panelStyle`): a bottom dialog kept to a width on a wide window (`{ maxWidth: '540' }`). `.frame()` is the centred card's width and ceiling.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `popover`

- `Popover(...content: Child[])`: A popover of `content`, presented with `.popover(isPresented, Popover(...))` on the view it is anchored on: Bloom's `Popover`, that view its trigger.

Modifiers:

- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: The popover's accessible name.
- [`.side()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#side) `(side: FloatingSide, align?: FloatingAlign)`: The side of the view it opens on, and its alignment along that side.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.sideOffset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sideoffset) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between the view and the popover.
- [`.alignOffset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#alignoffset) `(value: PaddingToken | Points)`: A shift along the side it opens on (negative toward the start).
- [`.frame()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#frame) `(value: PopoverFrame)`: Its widths and its height ceiling.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `connectionStatus`

- `ConnectionStatus()`: Bloom's connection toasts: an outage, the reconnection, the network back. It draws nothing in place; place it once, near the app's root.

Modifiers:

- [`.offlineMessage()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#offlinemessage) `(value: string)`: What the offline toast says (Bloom's localized words without it).
- [`.reconnectingMessage()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#reconnectingmessage) `(value: string)`
- [`.restoredMessage()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#restoredmessage) `(value: string)`
- [`.reconnectingDelay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#reconnectingdelay) `(value: DurationName)`: How long an outage lasts before the reconnecting toast shows: a duration token (`'3000'` by default).
- [`.restoredDuration()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#restoredduration) `(value: DurationName)`: How long the restored toast stays: a duration token (`'2500'` by default).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `menuContent`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `menuActions`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `header`

- `Header(title?: string | ViewNode)`: The page header, or the navigation bar (`.presentation('navigation')`), titled `title`: a text, or a view drawn in the title's place as it is.

Modifiers:

- [`.titleDisplay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#titledisplay) `(value: TitleDisplay)`: `large`: the title at the ramp's title step; `inline` (default): the bar's own.
- [`.subtitle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#subtitle) `(value: string | ViewNode | undefined)`: A line under the title: a text, or a view drawn there as it is.
- [`.toolbar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toolbar) `(...value: Child[])`: Trailing items, in reading order.
- [`.toolbarLeading()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toolbarleading) `(...value: Child[])`: Leading items, after the back button.
- [`.onBack()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onback) `(value: (() => void) | null | undefined)`: The back button's action; `null` shows none (a top-level destination).
- [`.scrollEdge()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolledge) `(value: ScrollEdge)`: How the edge meets the content under it: `auto` fades in as it scrolls.
- [`.scrollY()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolly) `(value: ScrollOffset | undefined)`: The page's scroll offset, when the header is not in a Bloom `Screen`.
- [`.presentation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presentation) `(value: HeaderPresentation)`: `floating` (default) islands, a `bar`, or the compact `navigation` bar.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.headingLevel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headinglevel) `(value: HeadingLevel)`: The title's heading level (the web's `aria-level`); 1 by default. The system bar has none.
- [`.backLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#backlabel) `(value: string | undefined)`: The back button's name for assistive technology; Bloom's "Back" by default.
- [`.inPage()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#inpage) `(value?: boolean)`: Bloom's `PageHeader` on iOS too, even in a native stack (no system bar).
- [`.safeArea()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safearea) `(value?: boolean)`: Pads its top by the safe-area inset (Bloom's default: on native, not on the web).
- [`.titleAlign()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#titlealign) `(value: TitleAlign)`: The title after the back button (`start`, the default) or centred on the bar.
- [`.accessory()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#accessory) `(value: Child)`: Full-width chrome under the title row (tabs, a toolbar).
- [`.tone()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tone) `(value: HeaderTone)`: `onImage`: light title and text over media.
- [`.scrimColor()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrimcolor) `(value: ColorToken | AppToken<'color'>)`: A colour role (painted from the theme, at an opacity too), or an app's own colour as written.
- [`.scrimFalloff()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrimfalloff) `(value: ScrimFalloff)`
- [`.titleReveal()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#titlereveal) `(value: TitleReveal)`: When the title shows: `auto` (default), `always`, `onScroll`, `onDock`.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `bottomBar`

- `BottomBar(...tabs: (TabView | false | null | undefined | readonly TabView[])[])`: The bottom bar: its tabs, in order.

Modifiers:

- [`.selection()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selection) `(value: Binding<string>)`: The `id` of the selected tab: a binding, a tab's press writing it.
- [`.action()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#action) `(value: Child)`: The action beside the tabs: a `FAB`.
- [`.minimize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minimize) `(value: Progress | undefined)`: Folds the bar as the page scrolls (Bloom `Screen`'s collapse progress).
- [`.progress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#progress) `(value: Progress | undefined)`: A swipe pager's continuous selection, in tab-index space.
- [`.hide()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hide) `(value: Progress | undefined)`: The bar's presence, 0 (shown) to 1 (gone): it slides off its edge as a pager leaves its pages.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `tab`

- `Tab(label: string, icon: IconComponent)`: A tab of a `BottomBar`: its label and glyph. Its `.id()` is what the bar selects.

Modifiers:

- [`.activeIcon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#activeicon) `(value: IconComponent)`: The selected glyph, when the set draws selection by shape (an outline that fills).
- [`.badge()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#badge) `(value: number | undefined)`: A count over the glyph's corner; nothing at 0.
- [`.id()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#id) `(value: string)`: The tab's identity: what the bar's `selection` speaks.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `action`

- `Action(icon: IconComponent, label: string, options: ActionOptions = {})`: Bloom `EngagementAction`: one glyph (and count) in an action bar. `label` names it for assistive technology.

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [engagementTone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#engagementtone), `'primary'`, `'vivid'`, `'secondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'success'`, `'warning'`, `'error'`, `'info'`

Modifiers:

- [`.count()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#count) `(value: number | null | undefined)`
- [`.active()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#active) `(value: boolean | undefined)`
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: EngagementTone)`

And every universal modifier.

### `actionBar`

- `ActionBar(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `EngagementBar`: leading actions in reading order; `.trailing(...)` pushes the rest to the far edge.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [controlSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#controlsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`
- `.tint()`: [engagementBarTone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#engagementbartone), `'default'`, `'onImage'`

Modifiers:

- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(...value: Child[])`
- [`.countFormat()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#countformat) `(value: (count: number) => string)`
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`
- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: `horizontal` (default), or `vertical`: a video's column of actions.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: EngagementBarTone)`: `default`, or `onImage`: legible over artwork.
- [`.showZeroCounts()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#showzerocounts) `(value?: boolean)`: Draws a count of 0 (a video's actions); by default a zero count is hidden.

And every universal modifier.

### `actionGroup`

- `ActionGroup(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `EngagementGroup`: adjacent actions that read as one control.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `lottie`

- `Lottie(source: string)`: A Lottie animation (a URL), drawn by Bloom's `Sticker`: the one Lottie path, paused while its screen is not in front. `.fallback(Image(still, label))` is the still it shows while loading, where it cannot play, and under reduced motion; `.frame({ width })` its square edge.

Modifiers:

- [`.loop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#loop) `()`: Plays again from the start each time it ends.
- [`.paused()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#paused) `(value: boolean)`
- [`.fallback()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fallback) `(value: Child)`: What draws in its place where it cannot play (reduced motion, no player).
- [`.decorative()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#decorative) `()`: Hidden from assistive technology: its container already names it (a sticker message's press).

And every universal modifier.

### `card`

- `Card(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Card`, holding its parts or any view.

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [tone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tone), `'neutral'`, `'accent'`, `'vivid'`, `'support'`, `'action'`, `'success'`, `'warning'`, `'danger'`, `'info'`
- `.style()`: [appearance](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#appearance), `'solid'`, `'subtle'`, `'outline'`, `'plain'`

Modifiers:

- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: BloomAppearance)`
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: BloomTone)`
- [`.radius()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#radius) `(value: CardRadius)`: Its corner: a corner rung of Bloom's radius scale (`lg`), `panel` or `concentric`.
- [`.elevation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#elevation) `(value: CardElevation)`: Its lift: `none`, or an elevation role (`s`, `m`, `floating`).
- [`.outline()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#outline) `(value: CardBorder)`: Its own stroke over its appearance's: `none`, `hairline`, `thin` or `medium`.
- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: Its content laid out side by side (`horizontal`) or one above another (the default), as a stack's.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between its children, as a stack's.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: Alignment)`: Its children across its axis, as a stack's.
- [`.justify()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#justify) `(value: Justify)`: Its children along its axis, as a stack's.

And every universal modifier.

### `row`

- `Row(title?: string)`: Bloom `Item`, a list row titled `title`.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [controlSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#controlsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`
- `.density()`: `'comfortable'`, `'compact'`

Modifiers:

- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`
- [`.leadingView()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#leadingview) `(...value: Child[])`: What sits before the text (an avatar, an icon).
- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(...value: Child[])`: What sits after the text (a value, a control, a chevron).
- [`.lines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#lines) `(value: number)`: The title's lines (one by default).
- [`.destructive()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#destructive) `()`
- [`.selected()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selected) `(value?: boolean)`: The chosen row of a list.
- [`.active()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#active) `(value: boolean | undefined)`: The row of the page on screen (a sidebar's current destination).
- [`.highlighted()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#highlighted) `(value?: boolean)`: Lit as the keyboard's current row (a command list's cursor).
- [`.density()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#density) `(value: Density<'row'>)`: Its rhythm: the touch list's (`comfortable`) or a desktop form's (`compact`).

And every universal modifier.

### `groupedSection`

- `GroupedSection(...items: Child[])`: Bloom `GroupedSection`: its rows, in order.

Token sets:

- `.density()`: `'comfortable'`, `'compact'`

- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: GroupedSectionStyle)`: `filled` (a card) or `plain` (rows on the page).
- [`.separator()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#separator) `(value: GroupedSeparator)` (here: [groupedSeparator](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#groupedseparator)): What sets the rows apart: `space`, a `line`, or `none`.
- [`.radius()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#radius) `(value: CardRadius)`
- [`.density()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#density) `(value: Density<'groupedSection'>)`: Its rows' rhythm, reaching every row in it.

And every universal modifier.

### `groupedSectionItem`

- `GroupedSectionItem(title: string)`: A row of a `GroupedSection`, titled `title`.

Modifiers:

- [`.leadingView()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#leadingview) `(...value: Child[])`: Its icon (a `GroupedSectionIcon`, an `Icon`, an avatar).
- [`.description()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#description) `(value: string)`
- [`.value()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#value) `(value: string)`: The current value, at the trailing edge.
- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(...value: Child[])`: A control at the trailing edge (a `Toggle`).
- [`.chevron()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#chevron) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.lines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#lines) `(value: number)`: The title's lines.
- [`.wrapControl()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#wrapcontrol) `(value?: boolean)`: A wide trailing control wraps below the title, keeping at least 140 points for the text.
- [`.descriptionLines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#descriptionlines) `(value: number)`: The description's lines: 2 by default, `0` never truncates.
- [`.destructive()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#destructive) `()`
- [`.disabled()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#disabled) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.onPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onpress) `(value: Press | undefined)`
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`
- [`.hint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hint) `(value: string | undefined)`
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.padding()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#padding) `(value: PaddingToken | Points)` or `(edge: Edge, value: PaddingToken | Points)`: A padding token, or points the view computes as it renders (a thread's indent times its depth, a bar's measured height and a gap), as `.frame()` takes; a number written out is a token (`'44'`).
- [`.frame()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#frame) `(value: FrameSpec)`
- [`.grow()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#grow) `()`: Takes the free space along its parent's axis.
- [`.shrink()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shrink) `(value: ShrinkPriority)`
- [`.alignSelf()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#alignself) `(value: Alignment)`: Its own place across its parent's axis, over the parent's `align`.
- [`.background()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#background) `(value: InkToken)` or `(value: ViewNode, alignment?: OverlayAlignment)`: A fill: a colour role, or a role at a named opacity (`'primary/10'`); or a view behind it, placed at `alignment` (centred by default).
- [`.border()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#border) `(value: InkToken, width?: BorderWidthName, edge?: BorderEdge)`
- [`.corner()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#corner) `(value: CornerName, side?: CornerSide)`: Rounds the box: a radius rung, `full`, or `concentric` (the radius of the shape it sits in, less the inset between them: a glass's, another concentric box's, a Bloom card's).
- [`.opacity()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#opacity) `(value: OpacityName)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `chip`

- `Chip(...content: Child[])`: Bloom `Chip`: its `Text` the label, an `Icon` before or after it the glyphs.

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [tone](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tone), `'neutral'`, `'accent'`, `'vivid'`, `'support'`, `'action'`, `'success'`, `'warning'`, `'danger'`, `'info'`
- `.style()`: [chipStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#chipstyle), `'solid'`, `'subtle'`, `'outline'`, `'plain'`, `'inverted'`

Modifiers:

- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: BloomTone | undefined)`: Its tone; `undefined` is no tone of its own (Bloom's neutral), so a tone chosen at render (`.tint(sensitive ? 'danger' : undefined)`) paints only while it holds.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: ChipSize)`
- [`.hue()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hue) `(value: ChipHue)`
- [`.toggle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toggle) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: A filter chip: on or off, through its binding.
- [`.selected()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selected) `(value?: boolean)`: Drawn chosen, its state owned elsewhere.
- [`.onClose()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onclose) `(action: () => void, label?: string)`: A remove button on the chip, named `label` for assistive technology.

And every universal modifier.

### `tabs`

- [`.fullWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fullwidth) `(value?: boolean)`: The tabs share the strip's width.
- [`.onReorder()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onreorder) `(value: (fromValue: string, toValue: string) => void)`: A tab moved (a drag, or Ctrl+Shift+Arrow on the web): the screen keeps the new order.
- [`.dragTranslation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dragtranslation) `(value: MotionValue)`: A swipe's travel between the tabs, which the underline follows.
- [`.pressOpenTab()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#pressopentab) `(value: PressOpenTab)`: What pressing the open tab does: `report` tells its value again, `reselect` takes its content back to the top (and reloads there), as social apps' inner tabs do (Bloom's `pressOpenTab`).

- [`.disabled()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#disabled) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(...value: Child[])`: A control beside its label, in its chrome, not part of its press.
- [`.onLongPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onlongpress) `(value: Press | undefined)` (here: a value): A long press: the tab's own menu.

### `embed`

- `Embed(content: ReactNode | ((render: (child: Child) => ReactNode) => ReactNode))`: A React element inside a tree: a third-party component, or a Bloom/app component the language has no node for yet. Pass a function to receive a `render` that draws language nodes in the current environment, for an element that takes them as children or props.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `group`

- `Group(...children: Child[])`: Several views as one, with no view of its own: React's fragment, SwiftUI's `Group`. Each child is drawn where the group is, as if it were there. As a `screen()` body's root it holds the screen's views, its screen modifiers the screen's chrome.

Modifiers:

- [`.id()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#id) `(value: string | number)`
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`: On a `screen()` body's root, the screen's own name; elsewhere it names nothing.
- [`.title()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#title) `(value: string)`: The screen's title: the navigation bar's on iOS, the page header's elsewhere.
- [`.titleDisplay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#titledisplay) `(value: TitleDisplay)`: `large`: the large title that folds into the bar as the page scrolls; `inline` (default).
- [`.subtitle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#subtitle) `(value: string | undefined)`
- [`.header()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#header) `(value: ScreenHeader)`: The screen's header: a presentation of its title and toolbar (`floating`, the default, `bar` or `navigation`), `false` for none, or a header node of its own (drawn in the page on every platform).
- [`.toolbar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toolbar) `(...value: Child[])`: The header's trailing items, in reading order.
- [`.toolbarLeading()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toolbarleading) `(...value: Child[])`: The header's leading items, after the back button.
- [`.onBack()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onback) `(value: (() => void) | null | undefined)`: The back button's action; `null` shows none (a top-level destination).
- [`.backLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#backlabel) `(value: string | undefined)`: The back button's name for assistive technology; Bloom's "Back" by default.
- [`.headingLevel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headinglevel) `(value: HeadingLevel)`: The title's heading level (the web's `aria-level`); 1 by default.
- [`.footer()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footer) `(content: Child, options?: ScreenFooter)`: The screen's own bar at its foot (a reply composer), over or under its content.
- [`.sheet()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sheet) `(open: boolean, content: () => Child, options?: SheetOptions)`: A sheet over the screen while `open`: Bloom's Dialog, from the bottom and centred from `md`.
- [`.onReselect()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onreselect) `(value: (() => void) | undefined)`: Pressing the screen's destination while it is in front (after it scrolled back to the top).
- [`.screenKind()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#screenkind) `(value: ScreenKind)`: What the screen's body is (Bloom `Screen`'s `kind`): `document`, `form`, `collection`, `table` or `canvas`. A collection or a table scrolls by its own list (`content`), a canvas does not scroll (`none`).
- [`.screenScroll()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#screenscroll) `(value: ScreenScroll)`: Who scrolls the screen: `auto` (default) its own viewport, `content` the list in it (a `ScrollView`), `none` a bounded canvas the chrome sits around.
- [`.documentScroll()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#documentscroll) `(value?: boolean)`: Web: the window scrolls the page, and the chrome stays sticky in its column.
- [`.headerPlacement()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headerplacement) `(value: ChromePlacement)`: `overlay`: the header paints over the content and reserves no room; `inset` by default.
- [`.scrollEdge()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolledge) `(value: ScrollEdge)`: How the screen's header meets the content scrolled under it: `auto` fades it in as the page scrolls.
- [`.underlay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#underlay) `(value: Child)`: A layer behind the whole screen, header and footer included, never interactive (a wallpaper).
- [`.floatingAction()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#floatingaction) `(content: Child, options?: FloatingActionOptions)`: The screen's own floating action (a `FAB`): the content pads its end clear of it, and the shell's steps aside.
- [`.contentClearance()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentclearance) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The room the content keeps above the screen's bottom chrome; Bloom's 16 by default.
- [`.navigationScope()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#navigationscope) `(value: NavigationScope)`: `isolated` keeps the shell's collapse and navigation motion out of a modal screen; `inherit` by default.
- [`.colorPreset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#colorpreset) `(value: ColorPreset)`: The screen's own colour recipe, one of Bloom's presets (Bloom `Screen`'s `colorPreset`): its content, its panel and the shell around it, restored when it leaves.
- [`.seed()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#seed) `(value: string)`: The screen's colours from one RGB seed (`#rrggbb`, Bloom `Screen`'s `seed`, a colour the person chose): over `colorPreset`, the roles from Bloom's colour engine.
- [`.screenActive()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#screenactive) `(value?: boolean)`: Whether the screen is in front (Bloom `Screen`'s `active`): a retained screen (`false`) drives no scroll, chrome or reselect. `true` by default.
- [`.replacesBottomBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#replacesbottombar) `(value?: boolean)`: The screen's own bar takes the place of the app's bottom bar (Bloom `Screen`'s `replacesBottomBar`): a post's reply prompt with the tabs docked beside it. While the screen is in front the app's bar steps aside in one cut, on the UI thread, from the screen's first frame (before a footer that waits for the push to end mounts). `true` by default.
- [`.screenBackground()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#screenbackground) `(value: InkToken)`: The screen's own fill, behind its header and content (Bloom `Screen`'s `style.backgroundColor`, the surface its chrome is painted for): a role, which Bloom's theme gives as its colour, or an app colour (a reel's black).
- [`.reselectTop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#reselecttop) `(isAtTop: () => boolean, scrollToTop: () => void)`: Where the top is for a screen that drives its own scroll (a reel, whose top is its first slide), and how to go back there: reselecting it away from the top calls `scrollToTop`, and at the top `.onReselect()` (Bloom `Screen`'s `reselect`, its `isAtTop` and `scrollToTop`). Without it the scroller in front answers.
- [`.initialHeaderHeight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#initialheaderheight) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The header's height before it measures itself (Bloom `Screen`'s `headerHeight`, safe area included).
- [`.initialFooterHeight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#initialfooterheight) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The footer's height before it measures itself (Bloom `Screen`'s `bottomBarHeight`, safe area included).
- [`.colorScope()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#colorscope) `(mode: ColorScopeMode | undefined)`: The screen in a colour scheme of its own (Bloom's `BloomColorScope`, its `mode`, around the Screen with `asChild`): a dark reel or camera over a light app. `undefined` is the app's scheme, the scope still drawn, so a scheme that changes at render never remounts the screen.
- [`.keyboardAvoiding()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboardavoiding) `(fill?: FlexName)`: The screen kept above the keyboard: React Native's `KeyboardAvoidingView` around it, filling, padded on iOS and resized elsewhere, as React Native recommends. SwiftUI avoids the keyboard by default; React Native does not. It fills with React Native's `flex: 1`, or with a flex token (`'1'` is Tailwind's `flex-1`, `flex: 1 1 0%`, as `.flex()` writes it).
- [`.provider()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#provider) `(component: ScreenProvider)`: A provider around the screen (a React component that draws its children: Bloom's `HeaderDockProvider`, an app's own context the screen's header and content share). Each of these three wraps what the root's modifiers before it drew, in chain order, as SwiftUI's modifiers do: the last one is outermost.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `view`

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `appLayout`

- `AppLayout(navigator: NavStackView | TabNavigatorView)`: The app's root layout: Bloom's `AppShell` with the navigator in it, the navigation the shell draws from the `TabPagerProvider` above it (the sidebar's rows, the bottom bar), and the primary action.

Modifiers:

- [`.primaryAction()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#primaryaction) `(icon: IconComponent, label: string, destination: string)`: The prominent action: the bar's FAB and the sidebar's first row on Android, OxyOS and the web; on iOS, `destination`'s tab, drawn on its own surface. `destination` is a destination's name (a page of the pager).
- [`.bottomBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#bottombar) `(value: boolean)`: Whether the bottom bar shows on this route (the system tab bar on iOS draws itself).
- [`.shell()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shell) `(value: ShellOptions)`: The shell's own options (panels, layout mode, error boundaries); `navigator` is where the stack goes.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.shellLayout()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shelllayout) `(value: ShellLayout)`: The shell's semantic layout: `workspace`, `collection-detail`, `feed`, `deck`, `catalog` or `map-browser`.
- [`.shellPresentation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shellpresentation) `(value: ShellPresentation)`: How the shell frames its content: `continuous`, `framed` or `floating`.
- [`.panels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#panels) `(...value: PanelView[])`: The shell's panels, in order; a `Panel` without content holds the navigator.
- [`.destinations()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#destinations) `(...value: DestinationView[])`: The app's destinations (Bloom's `TabPagerProvider`): the pager's pages, the bar's tabs, the sidebar's rows.
- [`.onReselect()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onreselect) `(value: ((name: string) => void) | undefined)`: Pressing the destination in front again, by its name (Bloom's scroll to the top, then reload, by default).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `navStack`

- `Stack(...routes: (string | RouteView)[])`: A stack of routes. Each name is a route of the layout's directory, pushed over the ones before it; `Sheet(name)` presents one as a sheet. With no names, the directory's routes as expo-router finds them.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `route`

- `Sheet(name: string)`: A stack's route presented as a sheet over the one under it (iOS); pushed elsewhere.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `tabView`

- `TabView()`: The tabs group's navigator over the `TabPagerProvider`'s pages: the system tab bar on iOS (expo-router's `NativeTabs`), Bloom's swipe pager under the shell's bottom bar on Android and OxyOS, the routed page on the web.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `textField`

- `TextField(text: Binding<string>, label: string)`: A one-line text input: Bloom's `TextFieldInput` in its `TextField` shell. `label` names it.
- `SecureField(text: Binding<string>, label: string)`: A text input whose text is hidden (`secureTextEntry`); `.revealable()` lets the reader show it.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [controlSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#controlsize), `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`

Modifiers:

- [`.placeholder()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#placeholder) `(value: string | null)`: Shown while the field is empty; `null` shows none (not even the label, which Bloom draws in its place otherwise).
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`
- [`.invalid()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#invalid) `(value?: boolean)`: Painted as wrong (the error ring) and announced as invalid.
- [`.floatingLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#floatinglabel) `()`: The label sits in the field and floats above the text once there is some.
- [`.revealable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#revealable) `()`: A `SecureField` the reader can show and hide again.
- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: IconComponent)`: A glyph before the text.
- [`.prefix()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#prefix) `(value: string)`: Text before the input (a currency, a protocol).
- [`.suffix()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#suffix) `(value: string)`: Text after the input (a unit, a domain).
- [`.keyboard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboard) `(value: Keyboard)`: The keyboard it asks for.
- [`.inputMode()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#inputmode) `(value: InputMode)`: The kind of text it takes (React Native's `inputMode`, the web's `inputmode`).
- [`.inputRef()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#inputref) `(value: RefObject<ComponentRef<typeof TextInput> | null>)`: The ref of its text input itself (React Native's `TextInput`): to focus it or move its caret.
- [`.onSelectionChange()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onselectionchange) `(value: (event: TextInputSelectionChangeEvent) => void)`: React Native's `onSelectionChange`: the input's caret or selection moved.
- [`.autocapitalize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autocapitalize) `(value: Autocapitalize)`
- [`.autocorrect()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autocorrect) `(value: boolean)`
- [`.contentType()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contenttype) `(value: ContentType)`
- [`.textContentType()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#textcontenttype) `(value: TextContentType)`: iOS: what the text is (React Native's `textContentType`: `URL`, `emailAddress`, `oneTimeCode`…), which its autofill and keyboard offer; `.contentType()` is Android's and the web's `autoComplete`.
- [`.submitLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#submitlabel) `(value: SubmitLabel)`
- [`.onSubmit()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onsubmit) `(value: () => void)`: The return key's action.
- [`.autoFocus()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autofocus) `()`: Takes the keyboard's focus when it appears.
- [`.maxLength()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#maxlength) `(value: number)`
- [`.trailingIcon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailingicon) `(value: IconComponent)`: A glyph after the text (and after a suffix).
- [`.completion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#completion) `(value: string)`: The rest of a suggested text, drawn faintly after what is typed (Bloom's `TextFieldGhost`).
- [`.focused()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#focused) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Whether it holds the keyboard's focus: focusing or leaving it writes the binding, and setting the binding focuses or blurs it.
- [`.multiline()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#multiline) `(value?: boolean)`: Text over several lines, the field growing with it (React Native's `multiline`).
- [`.editable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#editable) `(value: boolean)`: `false`: the text is shown and selectable but takes no typing (React Native's `editable`).

And every universal modifier.

- [`.keyboard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboard) `(value: Keyboard)`: The keyboard it asks for.
- [`.inputMode()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#inputmode) `(value: InputMode)`: The kind of text it takes (React Native's `inputMode`, the web's `inputmode`).

- [`.onSelectionChange()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onselectionchange) `(value: (event: TextInputSelectionChangeEvent) => void)`: React Native's `onSelectionChange`: the input's caret or selection moved.

- [`.autoFocus()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autofocus) `()`: Takes the keyboard's focus when it appears.
- [`.focused()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#focused) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Whether it holds the keyboard's focus: focusing or leaving it writes the binding, and setting the binding focuses or blurs it.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `search`

- `Search(text: Binding<string>)`: Bloom `Search`: a search field over `text`.

Token sets:

Modifiers:

- [`.onSelectionChange()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onselectionchange) `(value: (event: TextInputSelectionChangeEvent) => void)`: React Native's `onSelectionChange`: the input's caret or selection moved.
- [`.autocapitalize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autocapitalize) `(value: Autocapitalize)`
- [`.autocorrect()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autocorrect) `(value: boolean)`
- [`.submitLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#submitlabel) `(value: SubmitLabel)`
- [`.onSubmit()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onsubmit) `(value: () => void)`
- [`.onClearText()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#oncleartext) `(value: () => void)`: Called when the clear button is pressed: the screen empties its text (and, with tokens, its tokens).

- [`.maxLength()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#maxlength) `(value: number)`: The most characters the field takes (React Native's `maxLength`).

- [`.focused()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#focused) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Whether it holds the keyboard's focus: focusing or leaving it writes the binding, and setting the binding focuses or blurs it.
- [`.editable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#editable) `(value: boolean)`: `false`: the text is shown but takes no typing (React Native's `editable`), as a search that is full; `.disabled()` is Bloom's disabled look.

And every universal modifier.

And every universal modifier.
