### `selectionCard`

- `SelectionCard(title: string, ...content: Child[])`: Bloom `SelectionCard`, titled `title`; its content is the body under its head.

Token sets:

- `.density()`: `'comfortable'`, `'compact'`

Modifiers:

- [`.toggle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toggle) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Chosen or not through its binding: a checkbox, one of several.
- [`.selected()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selected) `(value?: boolean)`: The chosen one of a set, its press the screen's: a radio.
- [`.description()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#description) `(value: string)`
- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: IconComponent)`: A glyph on a tile at its start.
- [`.indicator()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#indicator) `(value: SelectionIndicator)`: Its mark: `check` or `radio` (by default, what it is).
- [`.illustration()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#illustration) `(value: Child)`: A view above its head (an image).
- [`.density()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#density) `(value: Density<'selectionCard'>)`: `compact`: the head's tighter padding.
- [`.invalid()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#invalid) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.nativeID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#nativeid) `(value: string)`: Its id, for a description or a label elsewhere to point at.
- [`.describedBy()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#describedby) `(value: string)`: The id of what describes it (a field's hint or error).

And every universal modifier.

### `scrollView`

- `ScrollView(...children: Child[])`: The screen's scroller (Bloom `ScreenScrollView`): its children are the content, in a column, bound to the screen's header fade, bottom bar fold, reselect and keyboard.

Modifiers:

- [`.contentMargins()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentmargins) `(value: SpaceToken)` or `(edge: Edge, value: SpaceToken)`: Room around the content, inside the scroll (the content container's padding).
- [`.contentGrows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentgrows) `(on?: boolean)`: The content container grows to fill the scroll (`flexGrow: 1` on React Native's `contentContainerStyle`), so content shorter than the viewport (an empty state) can centre or sit at its bottom; `false` (an `items.length === 0` written as a boolean) leaves it the content's height.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between the content's children.
- [`.refreshable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#refreshable) `(value: Refresh, tint?: RefreshTint<ColorToken>)`: Pull to refresh: a binding a pull writes `true` (the app writes `false` when done), or `{ refreshing, onRefresh }`, a refreshing value and the action a pull runs (a query's `isRefetching` and `refetch`). `tint` paints the refresh control's `tintColor` (iOS) and `colors` (Android) from a colour role, as an app hands it its theme's colour, or each platform's own (`{ ios: 'primary' }`: Android's spinner keeps its look); the platform's by default.
- [`.onEndReached()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onendreached) `(action: (() => void) | undefined, threshold?: number)`: Called once each time the end comes within `threshold` viewports (2 by default); `undefined` calls nothing.
- [`.scrollIndicators()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollindicators) `(value: ScrollIndicators, axis?: Axis)`: The scroll indicators: `hidden`, `visible`, or the platform's (`automatic`, the default), on both axes or on the one `axis` names.
- [`.scrollDismissesKeyboard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolldismisseskeyboard) `(value: KeyboardDismiss)`: A drag `immediately` dismisses the keyboard, or drags it away `interactively`; `never` by default.
- [`.keyboardTaps()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboardtaps) `(value: KeyboardTaps)`: Whether a tap with the keyboard up reaches the content: `handled` by a control, `always`, or `never` (default).
- [`.contentView()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentview) `(build: (view: ScrollContentView) => ScrollContentView)`: The content laid out in a view of its own inside the content container, as Bloom's `ScreenScrollView contentView` lays it out: `build` sets that view's layout (`.contentView((v) => v.padding('horizontal', 'lg').spacing('md'))`). The content container then grows to the viewport and pads only the screen's chrome; the content margins and spacing go on the view, after its own.
- [`.safeAreaInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareainset) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode, spacing?: SpaceToken)`: SwiftUI's `safeAreaInset`: `view` pinned at the scroll's `edge`, and the content inset by its measured height (and `spacing`), so it scrolls clear of it: a filter bar over a list, a composer under a chat.
- [`.safeAreaBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareabar) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode)`: SwiftUI's `safeAreaBar`: a `safeAreaInset` that is a bar, with Bloom's scroll edge scrim under it, so the content fades as it scrolls beneath.
- [`.stickyIndices()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stickyindices) `(value: readonly number[] | undefined)`: The children that stay at the top once scrolled to it, by index (React Native's `stickyHeaderIndices`: a profile's tabs); `undefined` keeps none.

And every universal modifier.

### `bottomSheet`

- `BottomSheet(...children: Child[])`: A sheet from the bottom (Bloom `BottomSheet`), as a `.sheet()`'s content: its children are its body. Placed anywhere else it draws nothing.

Modifiers:

- [`.detached()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#detached) `(value?: boolean)`: Inset from the screen's edges with every corner rounded; full width with a rounded top by default.
- [`.dragIndicator()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dragindicator) `(value: DragIndicator)`: The drag handle at its top: `visible` (default) or `hidden`.
- [`.material()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#material) `(value: SheetMaterial)` (here: [sheetMaterial](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#sheetmaterial)): Bloom's optical `surface` (default) or the plain backing fill (`flat`).
- [`.scrollable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollable) `(value?: boolean)`: The body scrolls inside the sheet (default); `false` when the body owns its own list.
- [`.sizing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sizing) `(value: SheetSizing)`: `content` (default) fits the body's height; `fill` gives a list a bounded viewport.
- [`.interactiveDismissDisabled()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#interactivedismissdisabled) `(value?: boolean)`: A drag down does not dismiss it.
- [`.onDismissAttempt()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ondismissattempt) `(value: () => boolean)`: Asked before the reader's dismissal (back, the backdrop, Escape, a drag); `false` keeps it open.
- [`.manualActivation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#manualactivation) `(value?: boolean)`: The body's drag starts only at the top of its scroll and past 8 down (for scrolling content on Android).
- [`.footer()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footer) `(content: Child, options?: SheetFooter)`: A bar under the body, its height reserved; `viewport` keeps it at the screen's edge while dragged.
- [`.progress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#progress) `(value: Progress | undefined)`: The sheet's presence, 0 (closed) to 1 (presented), on the UI thread.
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: The sheet's accessible name.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `sidebar`

- `Sidebar(...rows: SidebarRow[])`: The app's navigation column (Bloom `Sidebar`): its destinations and its tree, in order.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [sidebarSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#sidebarsize), `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`

Modifiers:

- [`.selection()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selection) `(value: Binding<string>)`: The id of the selected row, a destination or a tree row: a binding, a pressed tree row writing it, and a destination that links (Bloom's `onNavigate`, which then takes over the link's own push).
- [`.header()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#header) `(value: ViewNode | false)`: Replaces the logo and account row; `false` draws none.
- [`.toolbar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#toolbar) `(...value: Child[])`: A row of controls under the header, kept in view.
- [`.footer()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footer) `(content: Child)`: What stays at its foot, under the rows.
- [`.variant()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#variant) `(value: SidebarVariant)`: `panel` (default) or the narrow `rail`.
- [`.surface()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#surface) `(value: SidebarSurface)`: `card` (default), `plain` or `docked`.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: SidebarSize)`: Its width and rows: `sm`, `md` (default) or `lg`.
- [`.collapsed()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#collapsed) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Folded to its icons: a binding, its collapse control writing it.
- [`.searchable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#searchable) `(value?: boolean)`: Its search field (shown by default).
- [`.themeToggle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#themetoggle) `(value?: boolean)`: Its theme toggle (shown by default).
- [`.logo()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#logo) `(value: SidebarLogo)`
- [`.account()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#account) `(value: SidebarAccount)`
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: Its accessible name.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`
- [`.mode()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#mode) `(value: Binding<string>)`: The key of the selected `SidebarMode` (its switcher under the header): a binding, a press or its shortcut writing it.
- [`.modePresentation()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#modepresentation) `(value: ModePresentation)`: `segmented` (default), the labelled control; `toggle`, a compact switch for two modes.
- [`.modesLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#modeslabel) `(value: string)`: The modes' accessible name.
- [`.team()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#team) `(value: SidebarTeam)`: The team: its card at the foot and the menu it opens (Bloom's `SidebarTeam`).
- [`.teamPlacement()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#teamplacement) `(value: TeamPlacement)`: `header`: the team is the switcher at the top and the account the card at the foot; `footer` by default.
- [`.plan()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#plan) `(value: SidebarPlan)`: The plan card at the foot, in place of the team card (Bloom's `SidebarPlan`).
- [`.onboarding()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onboarding) `(value: SidebarOnboarding)`: The getting-started card under the destinations.
- [`.query()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#query) `(value: Binding<string>)`: Its quick search's text, and what to tell when it changes (the app filters its own content with it).
- [`.onDrop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ondrop) `(value: (drop: TreeDrop) => void)`: A `draggable` tree row was dropped: where it landed, by id; the app moves it.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `navItem`

- `NavItem(label: string, icon: IconComponent)`: A sidebar destination: its label and glyph.

Modifiers:

- [`.id()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#id) `(value: string)`: Its identity: what `selection` speaks; its label by default.
- [`.activeIcon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#activeicon) `(value: IconComponent)`
- [`.badge()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#badge) `(value: string | number | undefined)`: A count or a word at its end.
- [`.link()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#link) `(href: string)`: Where it goes (Bloom's router opens it).
- [`.shortcut()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shortcut) `(value: string)`: Its keyboard shortcut, drawn at its end and bound on the web (`⌘1`).
- [`.secondary()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#secondary) `()`: Pinned at the sidebar's foot, under its other rows.
- [`.onPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onpress) `(value: Press | undefined)`
- [`.onLongPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onlongpress) `(value: (() => void) | undefined)` (here: a value)

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

Modifiers:

- [`.collapsible()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#collapsible) `(value?: boolean)`: Its heading folds it.

- [`.icon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icon) `(value: IconComponent)`

- [`.draggable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#draggable) `(value?: boolean)`: It can be dragged (the sidebar's `.onDrop` takes it): a long press lifts it on touch.

- [`.keepMounted()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keepmounted) `(value?: boolean)`: Kept mounted while hidden.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `pageFooter`

Modifiers:

- [`.pinning()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#pinning) `(value: FooterPinning)`: `absolute` (default) over the page, `sticky` or `document` in the web's flow, or `inline`.

### `scrollArea`

- `ScrollArea(...children: Child[])`: A scroll of its own with Bloom's bar (Bloom `ScrollArea`); its children are its content.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [scrollbarSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#scrollbarsize), `'sm'`, `'md'`

Modifiers:

- [`.contentMargins()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentmargins) `(value: SpaceToken)` or `(edge: Edge, value: SpaceToken)`: Room around the content, inside the scroll (the content container's padding).
- [`.contentGrows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentgrows) `(on?: boolean)`: The content container grows to fill the scroll (`flexGrow: 1` on React Native's `contentContainerStyle`), so content shorter than the viewport (an empty state) can centre or sit at its bottom; `false` (an `items.length === 0` written as a boolean) leaves it the content's height.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between the content's children.
- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: `horizontal` scrolls across; `vertical` by default.
- [`.scrollbar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollbar) `(value: ScrollbarVisibility)`: When its bar shows: as it scrolls (`scroll`, default), on `hover`, `always`, `never`.
- [`.scrollbarSide()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollbarside) `(value: ScrollbarSide)`: The inline edge a vertical bar sits on (`end` by default); the web's, as the platform draws a native one.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: ScrollbarSize)`: The bar's thickness: `sm` (default) or `md`.
- [`.scrollDismissesKeyboard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolldismisseskeyboard) `(value: KeyboardDismiss)`: A drag `immediately` dismisses the keyboard, or drags it away `interactively`; `never` by default.
- [`.keyboardTaps()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboardtaps) `(value: KeyboardTaps)`: Whether a tap with the keyboard up reaches the content: `handled` by a control, `always`, or `never` (default).
- [`.safeAreaInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareainset) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode, spacing?: SpaceToken)`: SwiftUI's `safeAreaInset`: `view` pinned at the scroll's `edge`, and the content inset by its measured height (and `spacing`), so it scrolls clear of it: a filter bar over a list, a composer under a chat.
- [`.safeAreaBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareabar) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode)`: SwiftUI's `safeAreaBar`: a `safeAreaInset` that is a bar, with Bloom's scroll edge scrim under it, so the content fades as it scrolls beneath.

And every universal modifier.

### `scrollSurface`

- `ScrollSurface(...children: Child[])`: A scroll on a surface, its ends fading into it (Bloom `ScrollSurface`).

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [scrollbarSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#scrollbarsize), `'sm'`, `'md'`
- `.style()`: [scrollSurfaceStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#scrollsurfacestyle), `'primary'`, `'secondary'`, `'full'`

Modifiers:

- [`.contentMargins()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentmargins) `(value: SpaceToken)` or `(edge: Edge, value: SpaceToken)`: Room around the content, inside the scroll (the content container's padding).
- [`.contentGrows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentgrows) `(on?: boolean)`: The content container grows to fill the scroll (`flexGrow: 1` on React Native's `contentContainerStyle`), so content shorter than the viewport (an empty state) can centre or sit at its bottom; `false` (an `items.length === 0` written as a boolean) leaves it the content's height.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between the content's children.
- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: `horizontal` scrolls across; `vertical` by default.
- [`.scrollbar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollbar) `(value: ScrollbarVisibility)`: When its bar shows: as it scrolls (`scroll`, default), on `hover`, `always`, `never`.
- [`.scrollbarSide()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollbarside) `(value: ScrollbarSide)`: The inline edge a vertical bar sits on (`end` by default); the web's, as the platform draws a native one.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: ScrollbarSize)`: The bar's thickness: `sm` (default) or `md`.
- [`.scrollDismissesKeyboard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolldismisseskeyboard) `(value: KeyboardDismiss)`: A drag `immediately` dismisses the keyboard, or drags it away `interactively`; `never` by default.
- [`.keyboardTaps()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboardtaps) `(value: KeyboardTaps)`: Whether a tap with the keyboard up reaches the content: `handled` by a control, `always`, or `never` (default).
- [`.stickyHeader()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stickyheader) `(value: Child)`: Kept at its top while it fits beside the footer.
- [`.stickyFooter()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stickyfooter) `(value: Child)`: Kept at its foot; chrome too tall to keep scrolls.
- [`.fadeHeight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fadeheight) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The depth of each end's fade (24 by default).
- [`.fadeBottom()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fadebottom) `(value?: boolean)`: Whether its foot fades too (it does by default).
- [`.fade()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fade) `(value: InkToken)`: The colour its ends fade into: a role or an app's own (the surface's by default).
- [`.matchSurfacePaint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#matchsurfacepaint) `(value?: boolean)`: The fade takes the paint of the nearest material, not the surface's colour.
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: ScrollSurfaceStyle)`: The surface it sits on: `primary` (default), `secondary` or `full`.
- [`.safeAreaInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareainset) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode, spacing?: SpaceToken)`: SwiftUI's `safeAreaInset`: `view` pinned at the scroll's `edge`, and the content inset by its measured height (and `spacing`), so it scrolls clear of it: a filter bar over a list, a composer under a chat.
- [`.safeAreaBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareabar) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode)`: SwiftUI's `safeAreaBar`: a `safeAreaInset` that is a bar, with Bloom's scroll edge scrim under it, so the content fades as it scrolls beneath.

And every universal modifier.

### `autoHideBar`

- `AutoHideBar(bar: Child, ...body: Child[])`: A bar that steps aside to a strip while collapsed (Bloom `AutoHideBar`): `bar`, then its body.

Modifiers:

- [`.summary()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#summary) `(value: Child | string)`: What the collapsed strip shows; a string is drawn muted.
- [`.collapsed()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#collapsed) `(value?: boolean)`: The owner's decision that the bar steps aside (a page scrolled down).
- [`.onReveal()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onreveal) `(value: () => void)`: The strip was pressed: the owner clears `collapsed`.
- [`.peek()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#peek) `(value?: boolean)`: Hovering the strip shows the bar over the body for a while (on by default).
- [`.barHeight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#barheight) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The bar's height (41 by default).
- [`.collapsedHeight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#collapsedheight) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The strip's height (20 by default).
- [`.band()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#band) `(value: InkToken)`: The band behind the bar: a role or an app's own (the fill of the surface it sits on by default).
- [`.peekDelay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#peekdelay) `(value: DurationName)`: How long the pointer rests on the strip before the bar peeks: a duration token (`fast` by default).
- [`.unpeekDelay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#unpeekdelay) `(value: DurationName)`: How long after the pointer leaves a peeked bar it collapses again: a duration token (`'250'` by default).
- [`.hideMotion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hidemotion) `(value: MotionToken)`: How it collapses: a motion token (Bloom's own ease-out over 150ms by default).
- [`.showMotion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#showmotion) `(value: MotionToken)`: How it comes back, revealed or peeked: a motion token (Bloom's own ease-out over 200ms by default).
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: Names the strip's button.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `resizeHandle`

- `ResizeHandle(onResize: (distance: number) => void)`: The separator between two panes that resizes them (Bloom `ResizeHandle`): `onResize` is told the distance along the resize axis from where the drag started.

Token sets:

- `.style()`: [resizeHandleStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#resizehandlestyle), `'grip'`, `'pill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: `vertical` (default) resizes widths; `horizontal` heights.
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: ResizeHandleStyle)`: `grip` (default), a panel edge's grip; `pill`, a bar centred on the gap.
- [`.thickness()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#thickness) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The depth of its hit strip, across the separator.
- [`.onResizeStart()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onresizestart) `(value: () => void)`
- [`.onResizeEnd()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onresizeend) `(value: () => void)`
- [`.onNudge()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onnudge) `(value: (distance: number) => void)`: A keyboard nudge (on the web, the arrow keys move it by 16).
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: Names the separator.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `sidebarMode`

- `SidebarMode(label: string, icon: IconComponent)`: A mode of the sidebar's switcher under its header: its label and glyph.

Modifiers:

- [`.id()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#id) `(value: string)`: Its key: what `.mode()` speaks; its label by default.
- [`.shortcut()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shortcut) `(value: string)`: Its keyboard shortcut on the web, shown on hover and bound by the sidebar (`⌥⌃1`).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `settingsSection`

- `SettingsSection(label: string | undefined, ...rows: Child[])`: A settings section: its label (none for an unlabelled card) and its rows.

Modifiers:

- [`.description()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#description) `(value: string)`: A muted line under the label.
- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(...value: Child[])`: An action at the header's end, bottom-aligned with the label (Bloom's `action`).
- [`.inset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#inset) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The label's inset (12 by default; Bloom's Tools page uses 8).

And every universal modifier.

### `settingsValue`

- `SettingsValue(text: string)`: A value shown in a settings row's field.

- [`.muted()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#muted) `(value?: boolean)`: In the secondary ink (a truncated device id).

### `settingsSelect`

- `SettingsSelect(label: string, selection: Binding<T>, items: ReadonlyArray<SettingsChoice<T>>)`: A settings row's compact select: its name, the selection and its choices.

- [`.width()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#width) `(value: SpaceToken)` (here: [space](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#space)): The web dropdown's width; the native sheet sizes itself.

And every universal modifier.

### `datePicker`

- `DatePicker(selection: Binding<Date | null>)`: Bloom `DatePicker`: the day the binding holds (`null` for none), set on Apply.

Modifiers:

- [`.bounds()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#bounds) `(min: Date | null, max?: Date | null)`: Days before `min` and after `max` cannot be chosen; `null` leaves that end open.

- [`.presented()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presented) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Whether the popup is open, and what opening and closing it set.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: DatePickerLabels)`: Wording of your own for its buttons and month chevrons (Bloom speaks them in `locale`).

And every universal modifier.

### `timeField`

### `rating`

- `Rating(value: number | string | null | undefined)`: Bloom `Rating`: a rating on a 5 scale (a string is drawn as given; none draws `New`).

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [ratingSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#ratingsize), `'sm'`, `'md'`
- `.tint()`: [color](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#color), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`
- `.style()`: [ratingStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#ratingstyle), `'compact'`, `'stars'`

Modifiers:

- [`.count()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#count) `(value: number | string | undefined)`: The number of reviews; a string is drawn as given (`1.2k`).
- [`.countStyle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#countstyle) `(value: RatingCountStyle)`: `parenthesis` (default) draws `(128)`; `reviews` draws `· 128 reviews`.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: RatingSize)`: `sm` or `md` (default).
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: RatingStyle)`: `compact` (default): one star; `stars`: five, filled to the value.
- [`.showsValue()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#showsvalue) `(value: boolean)`: `false` hides the drawn number; the accessible name keeps it.
- [`.starSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#starsize) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The star's edge, apart from the text's size.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The stars and the value (and the count, unless `countTint`): over a brand colour.
- [`.starTint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#startint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The filled stars alone.
- [`.countTint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#counttint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The count alone.
- [`.emptyTint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#emptytint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The unfilled part of each star (`stars`).
- [`.fillValue()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fillvalue) `(value: number)`: What the stars fill to (`stars`), apart from the drawn value (a localised string).
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: RatingLabels)`

And every universal modifier.

### `ratingInput`

- `RatingInput(selection: Binding<number | null>)`: Bloom `RatingInput`: the chosen rating the binding holds (`null` while none).

And every universal modifier.

- `RatingBar(label: string, value: number)`: Bloom `RatingBar`: what the row measures and its position on the scale.

### `meter`

- `Meter(value: number, total?: number)`: Bloom `Meter`: a measurement within a known range (a score, a charge): `value` of `total` (1 by default).
- `ProgressBar(value: number, total?: number)`: Bloom `Meter` as a progress bar: how far a task has got (an upload, steps done).

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [color](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#color), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.valueText()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#valuetext) `(value: string)`: The reading a screen reader says (`4 of 5`); the share of the track in the locale by default.
- [`.indeterminate()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#indeterminate) `(value?: boolean)`: The amount is not known yet: a segment sweeps (a quarter of the ring turns) and it reports no value.
- [`.decorative()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#decorative) `()`: A bar or ring with no role, hidden from assistive technology: the drawing inside a control that names itself.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The filled part's colour role (the accent by default).
- [`.trackTint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tracktint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The rail behind the fill (neutral by default).
- [`.thickness()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#thickness) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The bar's height, or the ring's stroke.
- [`.fillDuration()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fillduration) `(value: DurationName)`: Web: how long the fill eases to a new value (native snaps); none by default.
- [`.barCorner()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#barcorner) `(value: Exclude<CornerName, 'concentric'>)` (here: [cornerRadius](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#cornerradius)): The track's and the fill's corner; half the thickness (a capsule) by default.
- [`.reveal()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#reveal) `(value: MeterReveal)`: The fill's entrance, which never changes the accessible measurement.
- [`.revealMotion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#revealmotion) `(value: TimedMotionToken)`: How the fill enters: a timed motion token (Bloom's 300ms ease by default).

And every universal modifier.

- `MeterRing(value: number, total?: number)`: Bloom `MeterRing`: a measurement as a ring.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [space](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#space), `'none'`, `'2xs'`, `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`, `'xl'`, `'2xl'`, `'3xl'`, `'4xl'`, `'5xl'`, `'1'`, `'3'`, `'6'`, `'10'`, `'14'`, `'28'`, `'30'`, `'36'`, `'38'`, `'44'`, `'48'`, `'56'`, `'64'`, `'72'`, `'76'`, `'80'`, `'96'`, `'120'`, `'128'`, `'140'`, `'160'`, `'192'`, `'200'`, `'224'`, `'240'`, `'256'`, `'260'`, `'280'`, `'288'`, `'300'`, `'320'`, `'360'`, `'380'`, `'384'`, `'400'`, `'420'`, `'440'`, `'448'`, `'480'`, `'512'`, `'540'`, `'560'`, `'640'`, `'672'`, `'720'`, `'768'`, `'1200'`
- `.tint()`: [color](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#color), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.valueText()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#valuetext) `(value: string)`: The reading a screen reader says (`4 of 5`); the share of the track in the locale by default.
- [`.indeterminate()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#indeterminate) `(value?: boolean)`: The amount is not known yet: a segment sweeps (a quarter of the ring turns) and it reports no value.
- [`.decorative()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#decorative) `()`: A bar or ring with no role, hidden from assistive technology: the drawing inside a control that names itself.
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The filled part's colour role (the accent by default).
- [`.trackTint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tracktint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The rail behind the fill (neutral by default).
- [`.thickness()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#thickness) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The bar's height, or the ring's stroke.
- [`.fillDuration()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fillduration) `(value: DurationName)`: Web: how long the fill eases to a new value (native snaps); none by default.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The outer diameter (56 by default); the stroke is drawn inside it.
- [`.backdrop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#backdrop) `(value: ColorToken)`: A disc under the whole ring: the scrim that lets it read over a photo.
- [`.cap()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#cap) `(value: MeterCap)`: The arc's end: `round` (default) or `butt`.
- [`.content()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#content) `(value: Child)`: What is centred in it (a score, a glyph), hidden from assistive technology.
- [`.onStop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onstop) `(action: () => void, label: string)`: The ring stops what it measures: a stop square in its centre, the ring pressable, named `label`.
- [`.progress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#progress) `(value: Progress | undefined)`: A decorative ring's drawn share, 0 to 1, followed on the UI thread.

And every universal modifier.

### `statBar`

- `StatBar(label: string, value: number, max: number)`: Bloom `StatBar`: `label` over a bar filled `value` of `max`.
- `SplitBar(label: string, percent: number, leftValue: string, rightValue: string)`: Bloom `StatBar`'s split: `percent` (0 to 100) filled from the leading side, its two values under the ends.

Token sets:

- `.tint()`: [color](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#color), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The filled side's colour role (the accent by default).
- [`.trackTint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tracktint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The empty side's (neutral by default).
- [`.thickness()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#thickness) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The bar's height (6 by default).
- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(value: Child)`: What sits at the end of the label row (a trophy).
- [`.rangeLabels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#rangelabels) `(min: string | undefined, max?: string)`: The labels under a progress bar's ends (its range).

And every universal modifier.

### `table`

- `Table(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Table`: a `TableHeader` and a `TableBody`.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [tableSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tablesize), `'sm'`, `'md'`

Modifiers:

- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: TableSize)`: Row density: `md` (default) or `sm`.
- [`.minWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The table's narrowest width: a narrower container scrolls it sideways.

And every universal modifier.

### `tableHeader`

- `TableHeader(...columns: Child[])`: Bloom `TableHeader`: its `TableColumn`s.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `tableColumn`

- `TableColumn(label: TableContent)`: Bloom `TableColumn`: its header label.

Modifiers:

- [`.width()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#width) `(value: ColumnWidthToken)`: A fixed width, or `fit` to hug the content. Wins over `share`.
- [`.share()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#share) `(value: number)`: Its share of the remaining width (1 by default).
- [`.minWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The narrowest a shared column gets.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: TableAlign)` (here: [tableAlign](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tablealign)): Where its content sits: `start` (default), `center` or `end`.
- [`.sort()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sort) `(direction: TableSort, action: () => void)`: The header is a sort button: its direction (`none` draws no chevron) and what pressing it does.
- [`.prefix()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#prefix) `(value: Child)`: What sits before the label, outside the sort button (a select-all checkbox).

And every universal modifier.

### `tableBody`

- `TableBody(...rows: Child[])`: Bloom `TableBody`: its `TableRow`s.

Modifiers:

- [`.emptyState()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#emptystate) `(value: TableContent)`: What fills a 160 band with no rows (a string in the muted body type).

And every universal modifier.

### `tableRow`

- `TableRow(...cells: Child[])`: Bloom `TableRow`: its `TableCell`s.

Modifiers:

- [`.selected()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selected) `(value: boolean)`: `true` paints the row selected; either value announces the selection.

And every universal modifier.

### `tableCell`

- `TableCell(content: TableContent)`: Bloom `TableCell`: a string or number in the table's body type, or a view.

Modifiers:

- [`.width()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#width) `(value: ColumnWidthToken)`: A fixed width, or `fit` to hug the content. Wins over `share`.
- [`.share()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#share) `(value: number)`: Its share of the remaining width (1 by default).
- [`.minWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The narrowest a shared column gets.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: TableAlign)` (here: [tableAlign](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tablealign)): Where its content sits: `start` (default), `center` or `end`.

And every universal modifier.

### `listItem`

- `ListItem(title?: ListText)`: Bloom `ListItem`: a row, titled.
- `PersonRow(data: ListPersonData)`: Bloom `ListItem`'s person recipe: avatar, name, handle and its action.
- `CallRow(data: ListCallData)`: Bloom `ListItem`'s call recipe: the call log's row.
- `ConversationRow(data: ListConversationData)`: Bloom `ListItem`'s conversation recipe: the chat list's row.

Token sets:

- `.density()`: `'comfortable'`, `'compact'`

Modifiers:

- [`.supporting()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#supporting) `(value: ListText)`: The line under the title.
- [`.supportingLines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#supportinglines) `(value: SupportingLines)`: How many lines it takes: 1 to 3, or `adaptive` to the row's width.
- [`.prefix()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#prefix) `(value: Child)`: What leads the row (an avatar, an icon).
- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(value: Child)`: What ends the row (a time, a chevron).
- [`.content()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#content) `(value: Child)`: A body of its own in place of the title and supporting line, in the row's geometry.
- [`.footnote()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footnote) `(value: Child)`: What sits under the text (a progress, chips).
- [`.attachments()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#attachments) `(value: Child)`: Media under the row's text.
- [`.density()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#density) `(value: Density<'listItem'>)`: `comfortable` or `compact` rows.
- [`.compactPadding()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#compactpadding) `(value: SpaceToken)`: A compact row's own vertical padding.
- [`.active()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#active) `(value: boolean | undefined)`: The current row (the open conversation).
- [`.link()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#link) `(href: string)`: A link: an anchor on the web, opened on native unless `.onPress` is set.
- [`.onPrefetch()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onprefetch) `(value: () => void)`: Warm what the row opens (on hover, on press in).
- [`.checkable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#checkable) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: A checkbox that selects the row.
- [`.selecting()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selecting) `(value: boolean)`: Selection mode: every row shows its checkbox.
- [`.selectionLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selectionlabel) `(value: string)`: The checkbox's name.
- [`.rowActions()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#rowactions) `(actions: readonly ListAction[], onAction: (key: string) => void)`: The row's trailing action buttons, and what choosing one does (by key).
- [`.swipeActions()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#swipeactions) `(actions: SwipeRowActions, full?: SwipeRowProps['fullSwipeAction'])`: Actions revealed by a swipe, and the one a full swipe runs.
- [`.swipeEnabled()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#swipeenabled) `(value: boolean)`: `false` keeps the row's swipe actions from a swipe (they stay in its menu).
- [`.contextMenu()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contextmenu) `(value: Child)`: The menu a right-click or a long press opens on the row.

And every universal modifier.

### `list`

- `List(items: readonly ListEntry[])`: Bloom `List`: its rows (Bloom's `ListEntry` data, an `id` each), or `sections` of them.
- `SectionedList(sections: readonly ListSection[])`: Bloom `List` in sections.

Modifiers:

- [`.separator()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#separator) `(value: ListSeparator)` (here: [listSeparator](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#listseparator)): Between rows: `none`, a full `line`, or an `inset` line (default).
- [`.scrollable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollable) `(value?: boolean)`: The list scrolls itself (it is laid out in the page by default).
- [`.scrollHeight()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollheight) `(value: SpaceToken)`: A scrolling list's viewport height.
- [`.stickyHeaders()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stickyheaders) `(value?: boolean)`: Section titles stick while their rows scroll.
- [`.sectionIndex()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sectionindex) `(value: NonNullable<import('@oxy.so/bloom/list').ListProps['sectionIndex']>)`: The index down the side that jumps between sections.
- [`.headerContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headercontent) `(value: Child)`
- [`.footerContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footercontent) `(value: Child)`
- [`.emptyState()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#emptystate) `(value: Child)`: What shows with no rows.
- [`.loading()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#loading) `(value?: boolean)`: Placeholder rows while loading (`count` of them).
- [`.loadingRows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#loadingrows) `(value: number)`
- [`.safeAreaInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareainset) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode, spacing?: SpaceToken)`: A view pinned at the list's top or bottom, its scroller's content padded by its height (SwiftUI's `safeAreaInset`).
- [`.safeAreaBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareabar) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode)`: A `safeAreaInset` that is a bar, with Bloom's scroll edge scrim under it (SwiftUI's `safeAreaBar`).

And every universal modifier.

### `virtualList`

- `VirtualList(data: readonly T[] | null | undefined, row: VirtualListRowFn<T>)`: Bloom `VirtualList`: `row` draws each item from FlashList's `renderItem` argument (`{ item, index }`), as a view or as the element FlashList's own `renderItem` returns; keep it stable (module scope, or a `useCallback`): the list's `renderItem` keeps its identity while `row` does.

Modifiers:

- [`.contentMargins()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentmargins) `(value: SpaceToken)` or `(edge: Edge, value: SpaceToken)`: Room around the rows inside the scroll: `contentContainerStyle`'s padding, on every edge or one (a later margin of an edge replaces the earlier).
- [`.contentGrows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentgrows) `(on?: boolean)`: The content container grows to fill the list (`flexGrow: 1`): an empty state centres in it.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between the rows: `contentContainerStyle`'s `gap`.
- [`.itemKey()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#itemkey) `(value: (item: T, index: number) => string)`: Each item's key (its index by default).
- [`.itemType()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#itemtype) `(value: (item: T, index: number) => string | number | undefined)`: Each item's recycling type (FlashList's `getItemType`).
- [`.estimatedItemSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#estimateditemsize) `(value: number)`: The engine's first estimate of a row's height, in px.
- [`.columns()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#columns) `(value: number)`: Items per row.
- [`.itemLayout()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#itemlayout) `(value: VirtualListItemLayout<T>)`: How many of the columns an item takes: the function sets `layout.span` (Bloom's `overrideItemLayout`; 1 by default), so a full-width row sits in the grid. FlashList and the web; the `FlatList` fallback gives every item one column.
- [`.onEndReached()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onendreached) `(action: (() => void) | undefined, threshold?: number)`: Called near the end, `threshold` viewports from it; `undefined` (nothing more to load) calls nothing, as `onEndReached={undefined}`.
- [`.refreshable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#refreshable) `(value: Refresh)` (here: a value): Pull to refresh: a binding a pull writes `true` (the app writes `false` when done), or `{ refreshing, onRefresh }`, a refreshing value and the action a pull runs (a query's `isRefetching` and `refetch`).
- [`.stickyIndices()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#stickyindices) `(value: readonly number[] | undefined)`: The indices whose rows stick to the top; `undefined` sticks none, as `stickyHeaderIndices={undefined}`.
- [`.restorationKey()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#restorationkey) `(value: string)`: Where its scroll position is kept across visits.
- [`.headerContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headercontent) `(value: Child)`
- [`.headerFitsContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headerfitscontent) `()`: The header is its content's height and the list's width: its container neither grows nor shrinks and stretches across (`ListHeaderComponentStyle`'s `flexGrow: 0, flexShrink: 0, alignSelf: 'stretch'`), so the engine never stretches it to the viewport and the row after it (a sticky one) starts under it.
- [`.footerContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footercontent) `(value: Child)`
- [`.emptyState()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#emptystate) `(value: Child)`
- [`.tuning()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tuning) `(value: VirtualListTuning)`: The engine's tuning knobs, honoured by the engine that knows them.
- [`.screen()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#screen) `(options?: VirtualListScreen)`: The list is its screen's scroller, without a `ScrollView` around it: the header's fade, the bottom bar's fold, the reselect and the insets bind to it (Bloom's `screen`, `{}` by default). It must be inside a screen.
- [`.scrollbar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrollbar) `(value: ScrollbarVisibility)`: When the native scroll indicator shows (`never` hides it); on the web the screen's scroller owns the bar.
- [`.scrollDismissesKeyboard()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#scrolldismisseskeyboard) `(value: KeyboardDismiss)`: A drag `immediately` dismisses the keyboard, or drags it away `interactively`; `never` by default.
- [`.keyboardTaps()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keyboardtaps) `(value: KeyboardTaps)`: Whether a tap with the keyboard up reaches the row: `handled` by a control, `always`, or `never` (default).
- [`.safeAreaInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareainset) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode, spacing?: SpaceToken)`: A view pinned at the list's top or bottom, the content padded by its height (SwiftUI's `safeAreaInset`).
- [`.safeAreaBar()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#safeareabar) `(edge: InsetEdge, view: ViewNode)`: A `safeAreaInset` that is a bar, with Bloom's scroll edge scrim under it (SwiftUI's `safeAreaBar`).

And every universal modifier.

### `listSelectionBar`

- `ListSelectionBar(count: number, total: number)`: Bloom `ListSelectionBar`: `count` of `total` selected.

Modifiers:

- [`.selecting()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#selecting) `(value: boolean)`

- [`.onClear()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onclear) `(value: () => void)`

And every universal modifier.

### `carousel`

- `Carousel(...slides: Child[])`: Bloom `Carousel`: its `CarouselItem`s.

Modifiers:

- [`.headerContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#headercontent) `(value: Child)`: Above the slides (a title; the arrows' place when they sit in the header).
- [`.footerContent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#footercontent) `(value: Child)`
- [`.loop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#loop) `()`: The last slide leads back to the first.
- [`.groupSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#groupsize) `(value: number)`: Slides one arrow press moves.
- [`.arrows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#arrows) `(value: ArrowsPlacement | false)`: Where the prev/next arrows sit (`overlay` by default), or `false` for none.
- [`.arrowsVisibility()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#arrowsvisibility) `(value: ArrowsVisibility)`: `hover` shows overlay arrows only under the pointer.
- [`.arrowsInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#arrowsinset) `(value: SpaceToken)`: How far overlay arrows sit from the edges.
- [`.hidesUnavailableArrows()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hidesunavailablearrows) `()`: Hide an arrow that has nowhere to go (kept, disabled, by default).
- [`.dots()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dots) `(value?: boolean)`: The position dots under the slides.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: CarouselAlign)` (here: [carouselAlign](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#carouselalign)): Where a slide snaps: `start` (default) or `center`.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The gap between slides.
- [`.contentInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentinset) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The room before the first slide and after the last.
- [`.page()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#page) `(value: Binding<number>)`: The slide shown, and what a move sets.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: CarouselLabels)`

And every universal modifier.

### `carouselDots`

- `CarouselDots(count: number, page: Binding<number>)`: Bloom `CarouselDots`: `count` dots, the binding's index the current one.

- `.style()`: [dotsStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#dotsstyle), `'default'`, `'onMedia'`
- `.shape()`: [dotsShape](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#dotsshape), `'pill'`, `'dot'`

Modifiers:

- [`.shape()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#shape) `(value: DotsShape)`: `pill` (the active dot stretched, default) or `dot`.
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: DotsStyle)`: `default`, or `onMedia` over a photo.
- [`.max()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#max) `(value: number)`: The most dots drawn; more slides scroll them.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: Pick<CarouselLabels, 'dot'>)`

And every universal modifier.

### `markdown`

- `Markdown(source: string)`: Bloom `Markdown`: `source` as blocks.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [markdownSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#markdownsize), `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`

- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: ColorToken)`: The stroke's colour (`success` by default).

And every universal modifier.

### `spinnerIcon`

- `SpinnerIcon()`: Bloom `SpinnerIcon`: the loader's glyph without its label or its track.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [iconSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#iconsize), `'2xs'`, `'xs'`, `'sm'`, `'md'`, `'lg'`, `'xl'`, `'2xl'`, `'3xl'`, `'10'`, `'11'`, `'13'`, `'14'`, `'15'`, `'17'`, `'18'`, `'21'`, `'22'`, `'40'`, `'44'`, `'64'`, `'96'`
- `.tint()`: [color](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#color), `'foreground'`, `'muted'`, `'textTertiary'`, `'primary'`, `'primaryText'`, `'onPrimary'`, `'secondary'`, `'onSecondary'`, `'tertiary'`, `'onTertiary'`, `'background'`, `'surface'`, `'popover'`, `'border'`, `'input'`, `'success'`, `'successText'`, `'warning'`, `'warningText'`, `'error'`, `'info'`, `'mutedFill'`, `'card'`, `'destructive'`, `'white'`, `'black'`, `'transparent'`, `'onSurface'`, `'accent'`, `'errorText'`, `'infoText'`, `'surfaceFill'`

Modifiers:

- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: IconSizeToken)`: Its width and height: an icon rung, a numeric side or an app token (Bloom's 26 by default).
- [`.tint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#tint) `(value: InkToken)`: Its colour (Bloom's `color`): a role painted from the theme, or an app's own colour (`defineTokens({ color: { onVideo: '#FFFFFF' } })`), drawn as written; the text's colour by default.

And every universal modifier.

### `contextMenu`

- `ContextMenu(content: Child, ...rows: MenuChild[])`: Bloom `ContextMenu` on `content`: `rows` are what it presents (`Item`, `Submenu`, `Section`, `Divider`, or one `menu()` content). Its box and its `.label()` are the view's: the area that opens it.

Modifiers:

- [`.actions()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#actions) `(value: MenuActionsView)`: What its tagged rows do: an `actions()` view, as a `Menu`'s.
- [`.presentedAt()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presentedat) `(value: Binding<ContextMenuAnchor | null>)`: Where it is open, when the screen owns it: a point (or a box) while open, `null` while closed. Opening it at a point of the screen's choosing is setting it; every dismissal sets it back to `null`.

And every universal modifier.

### `collapsible`

- `Collapsible(open: boolean, ...children: Child[])`: Bloom `Collapsible`: `children`, shown while `open` is true. What opens it is the screen's (a row, a button): the collapsible draws no trigger.

Modifiers:

- [`.revealMotion()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#revealmotion) `(value: TimedMotionToken)`: How it opens and shuts: a timed motion token (Bloom's 300ms ease-in-out by default).
- [`.revealsOnAppear()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#revealsonappear) `(value?: boolean)`: Mounted open, it opens from nothing (for content that arrives while the person watches).
- [`.keepsMounted()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#keepsmounted) `(value: boolean)`: `false` unmounts the content once it has shut (it stays mounted by default).

And every universal modifier.

### `dropZone`

- `DropZone(accept: readonly DropKind[], onDrop: (event: DropTargetDropEvent) => void, ...children: Child[])`: Bloom `DropZone`: `children`, taking drops of the `accept` kinds (`files`, `text`, `url`, `html` or a media type); `onDrop` receives what was dropped.

Modifiers:

- [`.fileTypes()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#filetypes) `(value: readonly string[])`: The media types of files it takes (`image/png`, `image/*`); files of any type by default.
- [`.dragActions()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#dragactions) `(value: readonly DragAction[], preferred?: DragAction)`: The actions a drop may take, and the one it prefers.
- [`.folder()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#folder) `(value: string)`: Where a native platform puts dropped files (a folder of the app's).
- [`.onDragEnter()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ondragenter) `(value: (event: DropTargetDragEvent) => void)`: A drag it would take came over it.
- [`.onDragOver()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ondragover) `(value: (event: DropTargetDragEvent) => void)`: That drag moved over it.
- [`.onDragLeave()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ondragleave) `(value: () => void)`: That drag left without dropping.

And every universal modifier.

### `mediaInsetBorder`

- `MediaInsetBorder(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `MediaInsetBorder`, over the views it holds (usually none).

Modifiers:

- [`.opaque()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#opaque) `(value?: boolean)`: The full-contrast border, to line up with opaque borders beside it (softened by default).
- [`.mediaCorner()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#mediacorner) `(value: Exclude<CornerName, 'concentric'>)` (here: [cornerRadius](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#cornerradius)): The corner of the media it edges, so the hairline follows it (Bloom's `sm` by default).

And every universal modifier.

### `factStrip`

- `FactStrip(...facts: Child[])`: Bloom `FactStrip`: its `Fact`s, in order.

Modifiers:

- [`.minItemWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minitemwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The narrowest a fact gets before the strip scrolls (`96` by default).
- [`.contentInset()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#contentinset) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The scroll track's inset at each end, so the first fact lines up with the page.

And every universal modifier.

### `fact`

- `Fact(label: string, value: FactText)`: A fact: what it is (`label`) and the fact itself (`value`: a string is drawn large).

Modifiers:

- [`.caption()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#caption) `(value: FactText)`: What sits under the value (the stars, "Years old").
- [`.onPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onpress) `(value: Press | undefined)`: Makes the fact a button.
- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string | undefined)`: The fact's one accessible name (the text it holds by default).
- [`.id()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#id) `(value: string | number)`: Its identity in the strip (its label by default).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `sortablePhotoGrid`

- `SortablePhotoGrid(photos: Binding<ReadonlyArray<SortablePhoto>>)`: Bloom `SortablePhotoGrid`: the binding's photos, in order; a move writes the new order.

Modifiers:

- [`.onRemove()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onremove) `(value: (id: string) => void)`: A photo's remove button, and what it does (by the photo's id).
- [`.onRetry()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onretry) `(value: (id: string) => void)`: A failed photo's retry, and what it does.
- [`.onAdd()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onadd) `(value: () => void)`: The add tile, and what pressing it does.
- [`.max()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#max) `(value: number)`: The most photos: the add tile goes at it.
- [`.addHint()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#addhint) `(value: string)`: The line under the add tile's label.
- [`.columns()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#columns) `(value: number)`: Tiles per row.
- [`.imageVariant()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#imagevariant) `(value: string)`: The image resolver's variant each photo is drawn at (a thumbnail's).
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: Partial<SortablePhotoGridLabels>)`

And every universal modifier.

### `choiceList`

- `ChoiceList(label: string, choice: Binding<Value>, options: readonly ChoiceListOption<Value>[], content: (option: Readonly<ChoiceListOption<Value>>, state: Readonly<ChoiceListOptionState>) => Child)`: Bloom `ChoiceList`: `label` names the whole, the binding holds the chosen option's value, and `content` draws each option (decorative: the option is the control, named by its `label`).

Modifiers:

- [`.axis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#axis) `(value: Axis)`: The options' arrangement and the arrows that move through it: `horizontal` (default) or `vertical`.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: Between options: `lg` by default.
- [`.optionBox()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#optionbox) `(value: ChoiceOptionBox)`: Each option's own box.

And every universal modifier.

### `videoPlayback`

- `VideoPlayback(...children: Child[])`: Bloom `VideoPlaybackProvider` around `children` (once, at the app's root).

Modifiers:

- [`.autoplay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#autoplay) `(value: boolean)`: Whether a visible video starts on its own (the viewer's preference by default).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `videoViewability`

- `VideoViewability(viewable: ReadonlySet<string> | ViewableKeysStore, ...children: Child[])`: Bloom `VideoViewabilityProvider` around `children`: `viewable` is the keys on screen (a set derived while rendering) or the store a list publishes them to.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `videoViewabilityScope`

- `VideoViewabilityScope(key: string, ...children: Child[])`: Bloom `VideoViewabilityScope` around `children`: visible as the item `key` is.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `hoverCard`

- `HoverCard(trigger: Child, content: Child)`: Bloom `HoverCard` on `trigger`; `content` is the card (named with `.label()`).

Modifiers:

- [`.presented()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presented) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Whether the card shows, and what showing and hiding it set.
- [`.openDelay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#opendelay) `(value: DurationName)`: How long the pointer rests before it opens: a duration token (`'400'` by default; the web's).
- [`.closeDelay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#closedelay) `(value: DurationName)`: How long it stays after the pointer leaves: a duration token (`fast` by default; the web's).
- [`.side()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#side) `(value: TooltipSide)` (here: [tooltipSide](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tooltipside)): The side of the trigger: `bottom` (default) or `top`.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: 'start' | 'center' | 'end')` (here: [floatingAlign](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#floatingalign)): How the card lines up with the trigger along that side.
- [`.padded()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#padded) `(value: boolean)`: `false`: the card draws no surface of its own (its content paints one).

And every universal modifier.

### `expandableText`

- `ExpandableText(text: string, expanded?: Binding<boolean>)`: Bloom `ExpandableText`: `text`, clamped; `expanded` holds whether it is open when the screen owns it. Its type and colour are the text environment's (`.font()`, `.weight()`, `.foreground()`), as a `Text`'s are.

Token sets:

- `.style()`: [expandableTextStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#expandabletextstyle), `'below'`, `'inline'`

Modifiers:

- [`.lines()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#lines) `(value: number)`: Lines shown while collapsed (3 by default; 0 never clamps).
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: ExpandableTextStyle)`: `below` (a review, a bio) or `inline` (a description that opens for good).
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: ExpandableTextLabels)`

And every universal modifier.

### `categoryBar`

- `CategoryBar(label: string, items: readonly CategoryBarItem[], selection: Binding<string | undefined>)`: Bloom `CategoryBar`: `label` names the tablist, `items` are its categories (`{ key, label, icon }`), the binding holds the selected key (`undefined` selects none). A press sets it, the selected one's included.

Modifiers:

- [`.trailing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailing) `(...value: Child[])`: Content pinned after the strip (a Filters button); it never scrolls.
- [`.spacing()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#spacing) `(value: SpaceToken)`: Between items: `3xl` (32) by default.
- [`.backdrop()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#backdrop) `(value: ColorToken)`: The surface behind the bar, which the web edge fades blend into (the page's by default).
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: CategoryBarLabels)`

And every universal modifier.

### `menubar`

- `Menubar(...menus: (MenubarMenuView | false | null | undefined)[])`: Bloom `Menubar`: `menus` in a row; one opens at a time. `.label()` names the bar.

### `menubarMenu`

- `MenubarMenu(title: string, ...rows: MenuChild[])`: Bloom `MenubarMenu`: `title` in the bar; `rows` are a `Menu`'s (or one `menu()` content).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `compositionBar`

- `CompositionBar(segments: readonly CompositionSegment[], selection: Binding<string | null>, hint: string)`: Bloom `CompositionBar`: `segments` left to right; `selection` holds the chosen segment's key (a press sets it); `hint` shows under the bar while none is chosen.

- [`.readoutFormat()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#readoutformat) `(value: (amount: number, percent: number) => string)`: The readout of the chosen segment, from its amount and its share (0 to 100).

And every universal modifier.

### `phoneInput`

- `PhoneInput(number: Binding<string>, title?: string)`: Bloom `PhoneInput`: the binding holds the number; `title` is its visible label (and name).

- [`.showsInfo()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#showsinfo) `()`: The info glyph after the title.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: BloomSize)`
- [`.invalid()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#invalid) `(value?: boolean)`
- [`.trailingIcon()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#trailingicon) `(value: IconComponent)`: A 20px icon after the input.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: PhoneInputLabels)`

And every universal modifier.

- `MediaFlightLayer()`: Bloom `MediaFlightLayer`.

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `mediaFlightHost`

- `MediaFlightHost(id: string, content: MediaSurfaceContent)`: Bloom `MediaFlightHost`: `content` (an image `{ uri }` or a video `{ kind: 'video', player }`) as anchor `id`.

### `notification`

- `Notification(title: string | Child)`: Bloom `Notification`: its title, a string or a view.

Modifiers:

- [`.description()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#description) `(value: string)`

### `socialButton`

- `SocialButton(brand: Exclude<SocialBrand, 'custom'> | SocialBrandConfig, title?: string)`: Bloom `SocialButton`: a built-in provider (`'google'`, `'apple'`…), or a brand of your own (its icon, name and colour); `title` replaces the default ("Continue with Google", in the app's language).

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [socialButtonSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#socialbuttonsize), `'md'`, `'sm'`
- `.style()`: [socialButtonStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#socialbuttonstyle), `'colorful'`, `'black'`, `'white'`

- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: 'md' | 'sm')`: `md` (36 × 300, default) or `sm` (32 × 250).
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: SocialButtonStyle)`
- [`.iconOnly()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#icononly) `()`: A circle with the logo alone; its title becomes its name.
- [`.fullWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#fullwidth) `()`: It fills its container instead of its fixed width.
- [`.intent()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#intent) `(value: SocialIntent)`
- [`.link()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#link) `(href: string)`: A link: an OAuth start is a navigation (an anchor on the web).

And every universal modifier.

### `gallery`

- `Gallery(items: readonly GalleryMedia[])`: Bloom `ZoomableMediaGallery` of `items`, presented with `.fullScreenCover(isPresented, Gallery(items))`.

Token sets:

- `.style()`: [galleryStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#gallerystyle), `'overlay'`, `'page'`

Modifiers:

- [`.page()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#page) `(value: Binding<number>)`: The page shown: where it opens, and what swiping sets (the first by default).
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: GalleryStyle)`: `overlay` (default) or `page`.
- [`.pageIndicator()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#pageindicator) `(value: GalleryIndicator)`
- [`.mediaCorner()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#mediacorner) `(value: Exclude<CornerName, 'concentric'>)` (here: [cornerRadius](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#cornerradius)): The media's corner, as its thumbnails have it; `full` is a circle at every size (an avatar).
- [`.nativeControls()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#nativecontrols) `(value: boolean)`: The platform's playback controls on a video page (off: a video opens as a moving picture).
- [`.videoOverlay()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#videooverlay) `(value: (media: GalleryVideo) => Child)`: Status or actions of your own over the active video.
- [`.sourceAnchors()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sourceanchors) `(value: readonly (string | undefined)[])`: The media-flight anchors (`MediaFlightHost` ids) its items open from and fly back to, by index: without one, an item fades in and out at the centre.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: Partial<ZoomableMediaGalleryLabels>)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `meetingScheduler`

- `MeetingScheduler(booking: Binding<MeetingSchedulerValue | null>, host: MeetingSchedulerHost, meeting: MeetingSchedulerDetails, timezone: string)`: Bloom `MeetingScheduler`: the booking the binding holds (a day and a time, or `null`), set by its "Send meeting"; `host` and `meeting` are its card, `timezone` its timezone row.

Modifiers:

- [`.bounds()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#bounds) `(min: Date | null, max?: Date | null)`: Days before `min` and after `max` cannot be chosen; `null` leaves that end open.
- [`.unavailable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#unavailable) `(value: (date: Date) => boolean)`: Days that cannot be chosen (weekends, holidays).
- [`.weekStart()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#weekstart) `(value: WeekStart)`: The grid's first column: `0` Sunday (the default) … `6` Saturday.
- [`.locale()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#locale) `(value: string)`: The BCP 47 locale its months, weekdays and Bloom's own strings speak; the provider's by default.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: MeetingSchedulerLabels)`
- [`.timeSlots()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#timeslots) `(value: readonly string[])`: The bookable times, 24h `"HH:mm"` (every half hour 09:00 to 18:30 by default).
- [`.hourFormat()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#hourformat) `(value: HourFormat)`: How its times are drawn at first: `24h` (default) or `12h`; the reader can switch.
- [`.onTimezonePress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#ontimezonepress) `(value: () => void)`: Makes the timezone row a button.
- [`.triggerTitle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#triggertitle) `(value: string)`: The trigger's text (Bloom's `Schedule a meeting` by default).
- [`.presented()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#presented) `(value: Binding<boolean>)`: Whether its popup is open, and what opening and closing it set.

And every universal modifier.

### `inlineMarkdown`

- `InlineMarkdown(source: string | readonly MarkdownInline[])`: Bloom `InlineMarkdown`: `source` (or Bloom's parsed runs) as spans, inside a `Text`: `Text(InlineMarkdown(reply.summary))`.

Modifiers:

- [`.streaming()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#streaming) `(value?: boolean)`: The text is still arriving: an unclosed span is drawn as it will close.
- [`.onLinkPress()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#onlinkpress) `(value: (url: string) => void)`: What a link does (opened outside the app by default).
- [`.plainRun()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#plainrun) `(value: (text: string) => Child)`: A view of your own for each plain run (mentions, tags); not inside links or code.
- [`.linkView()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#linkview) `(value: (link: InlineMarkdownLink) => Child | undefined)`: A link of your own; `undefined` keeps Bloom's.
- [`.suffix()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#suffix) `(value: Child)`: What follows the runs, in the line (a "Read more").

And every universal modifier.

### `dataTable`

- `DataTable(rows: readonly T[], rowId: (row: T, index: number) => string, ...columns: DataColumnView<T>[])`: Bloom `DataTable`: every row (already filtered), each named by `rowId`, in its `DataColumn`s. `.label()` names the table.

Token sets:

- `.size()`: [tableSize](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tablesize), `'sm'`, `'md'`
- `.style()`: [dataTableStyle](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#datatablestyle), `'table'`, `'inset'`

Modifiers:

- [`.summary()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#summary) `(value: TableContent)`: The count line under the title (`48 customers`).
- [`.sorting()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sorting) `(value: Binding<DataTableSort | null>)`: The sort, `null` unsorted: what a header press writes.
- [`.rowSelection()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#rowselection) `(value: Binding<readonly string[]>)`: A checkbox per row and a select-all: the selected rows' ids.
- [`.pageSize()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#pagesize) `(value: number)`: Rows per page (8 by default).
- [`.page()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#page) `(value: Binding<number>)`: The current page, from 1.
- [`.size()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#size) `(value: DataTableSize)`: Row density: `md` (default) or `sm`.
- [`.sizeToggle()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sizetoggle) `(value: Binding<DataTableSize>)`: The Normal / Compact control under the table, and the density it sets.
- [`.bordered()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#bordered) `(value: boolean)`: `false` drops the table's own border, inside a surface that draws one.
- [`.emptyState()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#emptystate) `(value: TableContent)`: What fills the empty band with no rows.
- [`.minWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The table's narrowest width: a narrower container scrolls it sideways.
- [`.style()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#style) `(value: DataTableStyle)`: `table` (default) or the dashboards' `inset` rows.
- [`.labels()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#labels) `(value: DataTableLabels<T>)`

And every universal modifier.

### `dataColumn`

- `DataColumn(id: string, header: TableContent, value?: (row: T) => DataTableSortValue)`: A column: its `id` (its sort key), its header, and the row's value (what it sorts by and shows).

Modifiers:

- [`.label()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#label) `(value: string)`: The header's name, when the header is not a string.
- [`.width()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#width) `(value: ColumnWidthToken)`: A fixed width, or `fit` to hug the content.
- [`.share()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#share) `(value: number)`: Its share of the remaining width.
- [`.minWidth()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#minwidth) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The narrowest a shared column gets.
- [`.basis()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#basis) `(value: SpaceToken)` (here: [space](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#space)): A preferred width that grows and shrinks in proportion with the other columns'.
- [`.align()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#align) `(value: TableAlign)` (here: [tableAlign](bloom-language-tokens.mdx#tablealign))
- [`.cell()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#cell) `(value: (context: DataTableCellContext<T>) => Child)`: Draws the cell (the row's value as body text by default).
- [`.sortable()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sortable) `(value?: boolean)`: Whether its header sorts (it does when the column has a value).
- [`.sortsDescendingFirst()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#sortsdescendingfirst) `(value?: boolean)`: The first press sorts descending (numbers and dates do by default).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

And every universal modifier.

### `sortableChipRow`

- `SortableChipRow(...chips: SortableChipView[])`: Bloom `SortableChipRow`: `chips` in the screen's order; `.label()` names the row.

Modifiers:

### `glassGroup`

- `GlassGroup(...children: Child[])`: A glass group around `children`: their glasses render, blend and morph together.

Token sets:

Modifiers:

- [`.blend()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#blend) `(value: SpaceToken)`: The distance at which the group's glasses blend into one shape (SwiftUI's container `spacing`; a stack's gap is `.spacing()`). iOS and Android read it.

And every universal modifier.

### `viewThatFits`

- `ViewThatFits(axis: Axis, ...candidates: Child[])`: The first candidate whose ideal size along `axis` fits the room it is given, else the last (SwiftUI's `ViewThatFits(in:)`): adaptation to content (a long translation, a count, a toolbar label) that a breakpoint `when()` cannot express. Each candidate is measured once more, out of sight, so keep them small and few.

Takes the universal modifiers.

### `toolbarGroup`

- `ToolbarGroup(...items: Child[])`: Toolbar items that share one background (Apple: group items that perform similar actions).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `toolbarSpacer`

- `ToolbarSpacer(kind: ToolbarSpacerKind = 'fixed')`: A gap between toolbar groups: `fixed` (the default, a small gap) or `flexible` (the free space).

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

### `confirmationDialog`

- `ConfirmationDialog(title: string, ...rows: MenuChild[])`: A confirmation dialog's title and its rows (`Item`s).

Modifiers:

- [`.message()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#message) `(value: string)`: The text under the title.
- [`.titleVisibility()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#titlevisibility) `(value: TitleVisibility)`: Whether the title shows: `automatic` (the platform's choice), `visible` or `hidden`.

- [`.cancelLabel()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#cancellabel) `(value: string)`: An explicit cancel row, with this label, where the platform draws none of its own.
- [`.testID()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#testid) `(value: string)`

Takes only the modifiers above (no universal modifiers).

- [`.portrait()`](bloom-language-modifiers.mdx#portrait) `(value?: boolean)`: A cached still instead of a live renderer: for a picker's thumbnails.
